  - [visual-system.md](visual-system.md), revision 7, `READY_FOR_WIREFRAME_DESIGN`: Stage 2B complete after explicit acceptance of all six visual-direction recommendations on 19 October 2026. The latest 21 October exact theme-neutral and Buy/Sell instructions override the previous white-only toolbar treatment via VIS-DEC-008, VIS-SURF-005, VIS-COLOR-016/017 and VIS-STATE-016; green/red candles and VIS-DATA-010 edges remain; domain semantics and theme colors remain unchanged; the subsequent explicit side-panel geometry override is recorded in UIS-PAT-013 and UISYN-MOD-005.

Stable IDs introduced here persist across revisions. `FR-*`, `BR-*`, `DATA-*`, `SEC-*`, `CONS-*`, `SCALE-*` and `OPS-*` refer to requirements; `DC-*`, `TR-*`, `INV-*`, `PRE-*`, `HIST-*`, `OP-*` and `POL-*` refer to the domain model; `CONTRACT-*`, `WF-*`, `CM-*`, `FAIL-*`, `CONC-*`, `EXT-*`, `RETRY-*`, `DIAG-*`, `SECR-*` and `DEFER-*` refer to architecture. Numeric ranges below mean inclusive ID ranges within that source, not new IDs.

# PART I: INTERFACE REQUIREMENTS, ACCESSIBILITY & ENVIRONMENT SCOPE

## 1. Interface Scope & User Summary

| User / Actor | Context | Supported Outcome | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|
| Local analyst | Sole desktop user, reading supplied or acquired Dukascopy history | Discover, inspect and compare complete available bar history; author and recover analysis | AUTH-01, FR-01–FR-18, FR-43–FR-44, FR-52, OP-01–OP-05 |
| Same analyst, practising historical trading | A retained historical run with one common information boundary | Make hypothetical decisions, manage independent hedged positions, understand reproducible USD results | FR-19–FR-23, FR-27, FR-29, FR-37–FR-54; OP-06–OP-14 |
| Same analyst, maintaining local work/data access | Data-access limitation, interrupted acquisition, save failure, restart or recovery | Understand what remains usable/durable and recover without silently changing original run inputs | FR-31–FR-35, FR-43–FR-46, FR-55–FR-56; CONTRACT-003/007/009 |

The initial catalog contains AUDJPY, EURGBP, EURUSD, GBPUSD, USDJPY, DAX, FTSE, NASDAQ, SP500 and XAUUSD. Preserve provider qualification and observed aliases, including DEUIDXEUR, GBRIDXGBP, USATECHIDXUSD and USA500IDXUSD. Offer exactly M1/M5/M15/H1/H4/D1/W1/MN1 and bid/ask, with bid default. No missing provider metadata is inferred from a similar asset. [DATA-02–DATA-03, FR-01/04/07, POL-01]

The application supports candles/line, provider-labelled volume, SMA/EMA/RSI/MACD/Bollinger Bands, the core drawing subset, one/two charts with explicit interval sync, named saved layouts, local image/own-run report export, historical simulation, required Dukascopy live-account data and M1/H1 recovery. Drawings include trendline, horizontal/vertical line, ray, rectangle, text, Fibonacci retracement, brush and long/short position measurement. Measurement is authored analysis and creates no order. [FR-05/11–FR-18/27/29/31–FR-35]

Excluded/deferred surfaces: scripting/Pine, alerts, actual broker execution/cash, margin/liquidation, extra slippage, external completed-run import, cloud sharing, mobile/touch support, more than two charts, tick/second/custom chart periods, automatic missing-tick downloads, volume profiles and unselected reference catalogs. Manual restoration of the application's own backup remains included. [FR-26/28/30/41/55, requirements Section 3, POL-12]

## 3. Required Information (`UIR-INFO-*`) & User Actions (`UIR-ACT-*`)

### Required information

| ID | User Need | Information / Meaning | When Needed | Authority / Sensitivity Notes | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UIR-INFO-001 | Recognize the actual analysis basis | Local/provider identity, period, bid/ask, candle/line and selected chart; requested versus adopted basis | Before / During / Outcome | No substitute product/midpoint or relabelling old bars | FR-02–FR-05/07/17; INV-01; CM-001 |
| UIR-INFO-002 | Know what history exists | Discovered earliest/latest usable bounds, gaps/invalid intervals, quality/producer assertions and input revision; loaded range separate | Before / During / On-demand | A span/COMPLETE flag/100k resident bars proves neither continuity nor archive end | FR-02/06/51–FR-52; DC-06; CONTRACT-001 |
| UIR-INFO-003 | Read a financial observation | Selected-bar event/bucket time, display timezone, OHLC/side volume, provider-volume limitation, study outputs or unavailable/warm-up reason | During / On-demand | Numeric access outside plot; formatting leaves stored values/UTC semantics intact | FR-05/08/10–FR-11/24; POL-01/10 |
| UIR-INFO-004 | Interpret supporting instruments | Watchlist/details' instrument, side, period, reference observation time, metadata and unavailable fields | During | Replay boundary applies; no latest fallback | FR-09/20; CONTRACT-012/020 |
| UIR-INFO-005 | Understand authored analysis | Layout name/identity, active chart, study instance/inputs/visibility, drawing target/type/anchors/hidden/locked meaning, labelled undo/redo action | Before / During / Outcome | Drawings belong to layout/instrument and can outdate replay cursor as user annotations | FR-12–FR-17; REL-04; AUD-004 |
| UIR-INFO-006 | Know the replay decision context | Run identity/basis, explicit cursor, play/pause/speed, processed decision boundary, retained versus current inputs and actual coverage limit | Before / During / Outcome | Cursor differs from hover/date range/wall time; all panels obey common boundary | FR-19–FR-23/40/45/53; DC-13/14; CONTRACT-020 |
| UIR-INFO-007 | Evaluate simulated entry terms | Direction/type, quantity/units, order/SL/TP levels, target instrument, executable side, estimated quantity/planned risk/fees and effective-after-boundary meaning | Before / During / Outcome | Direction, terms, validation and estimates refer to the same current entry draft; opposite direction requires explicit draft selection and fresh validation before submission. Entry buys ask/sells bid irrespective of chart side; estimates are not fills or guaranteed maximum loss | FR-27/39–FR-41/47/49/53; POL-04–POL-08 |
| UIR-INFO-008 | Interpret account and simulation settings | Fixed USD currency, initial balance, fixed/risk choice, positive-balance/SL requirements, fee basis/rate, unit/multiplier and price/quantity increments/minimum, identified metadata overrides and selected conventions | Before / On-demand / Outcome | Simulation-only; missing values explicit; current edits do not rewrite retained runs | FR-39/41/49–FR-50/54; DATAOWN-011; CM-005 |
| UIR-INFO-009 | Distinguish pending instruction from exposure | Draft/accepted/effective pending/triggered market/cancelled/filled facts; targeted position identity, direction, entry/remaining quantities and associated protection | During / Outcome | Accepted is not filled; opposite entries hedge; manual partial close is an explicit request | FR-38/40/48/53–FR-54; TR-21–TR-29 |
| UIR-INFO-010 | Explain a result | Event/time/side/price/quantity, entry/exit fees, USD results and metrics, historical FX/specification/convention basis, finer-bar/tick evidence and ASM-01/02 labels/limitations | Outcome / On-demand / Export | Recorded simulation evidence, not broker reconstruction; preserve original source precision | FR-29/37/41–FR-42/45/48; HIST-04–HIST-06; AUD-002 |
| UIR-INFO-011 | Resolve a paused event | Original event/instrument/time, missing gap/session/FX/specification/price prerequisite, last coherent prefix, permitted compatible resolution versus updated-data restart | During / On-demand | No unvalued fill, guessed rate or new-price resumption | FR-42/45–FR-46/50; CONTRACT-007; DIAG-004 |
| UIR-INFO-012 | Assess live/recovery condition | Data-only Dukascopy access availability; receipt versus event time; receiving/no recent receipt/proven closure/stale/failed/recovering; requested interval, qualified progress, provisional/final status and unresolved spans | During / On-demand | Provider delays have no guaranteed deadline; no credentials/account figures/raw responses in views | FR-31–FR-35/56; EXT-002–EXT-003; SECR-003; DIAG-002 |
| UIR-INFO-013 | Assess recovery and durability | Current working versus exact successfully saved version; pending/error/unknown publication; recoverable checkpoint and backup integrity/included run inputs | During / Outcome / On-demand | A coherent working fill may still be unsaved; unknown acknowledgement is not known failure | FR-44–FR-45/55; CM-003; FAIL-012–FAIL-013; DIAG-006 |
| UIR-INFO-014 | Understand a destructive consequence | Operation kind, selected layout/object/run, rewind destination and discarded suffix or replacement/deletion scope; drawings' independent scope; change of input basis on updated-data restart | Before confirmation / Outcome | Confirmation is version/scope-bound; cancel preserves original state; no automatic branch promised | FR-21/45/55; TX-005; SECR-002 |
| UIR-INFO-015 | Recover denied or failed operations | Sanitized reason, affected context, known failure versus uncertainty, supported next action and safe local correlation | During / On-demand | No bearer material, raw paths, full financial dumps or raw captured content | SEC-02–SEC-04; DIAG-001–DIAG-009; LOG-001–LOG-005 |
| UIR-INFO-016 | Verify a local output | Source snapshot/run/cursor, included analysis, basis/units/evidence labels; complete/failed/unknown artifact outcome | Before / Outcome | Backup, image and own-run report have different scopes; no credentials/hosted publication | FR-18/29/55; CONTRACT-010; FAIL-019/023 |

## 4. State Distinctions, Feedback, Errors & Safety

Each row specifies user-perceivable meaning, not a prescribed visual component. Independent conditions coexist: replay can be paused with an unsaved coherent working result while the current-data feed is recovering. Never collapse these into one overall success/error flag.

| ID | Category | Condition / Trigger | What the User Must Understand / Confirm | Recovery / Consequence if Confused | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UIR-STATE-001 | STATE | Discovery/loading/selection pending | Requested basis differs from adopted evidence; previous valid chart retains its own labels | Await or change selection; discard obsolete results | FR-02–FR-03; CM-001; FAIL-002; CONC-002 |
| UIR-STATE-002 | STATE | No search match, absent observation, invalid/unreadable data, warm-up or actual coverage end | Distinct reasons for absence; resident-range limit is loading rather than archive exhaustion | Correct query, inspect coverage, retry valid read; no zero substitution | FR-02/06/08/11/25/52; TR-19 |
| UIR-STATE-003 | STATE | Limited/invalid coverage, unexplained expected-session gap or known closure | Trading pauses at unresolved gaps; only evidence-established closures permit normal traversal | Keep readable history; explain unresolved interval and evidence | FR-46/51; POL-02; FAIL-009 |
| UIR-STATE-004 | STATE | Receiving/no recent receipt/stale/failed/proven closure/recovering | Receipt, price event, closure and access/failure are distinct facts | Inspect qualified status; reconnect/catch up without invented movement/demo substitution | FR-31–FR-35/56; EXT-002–EXT-003 |
| UIR-STATE-005 | STATE | Provisional current candle, completed eligible bar, final publication, superseded current revision | Period completion, validation and provider finality differ; finalization affects current/new-run history | Retain provenance and saved-run originals | FR-20/35/45/51; DC-09; CM-004/005 |
| UIR-STATE-006 | STATE | Preview/committed/invalid/cancelled analysis; locked/hidden selected object | Commitment and validity differ from visibility/lock and durability | Correct invalid input, cancel or unlock selected permitted object; no lasting cancelled preview | FR-10/12/14–FR-16; TR-07–TR-09 |
| UIR-STATE-007 | STATE | Unsaved/saving/exact-version saved/known save error/publication unknown | Only confirmed retained version is durable; later edits may still be unsaved | Reconcile publication, retain/retry correct version; crash can lose unsaved suffix | FR-44; CM-003; FAIL-003/012–FAIL-013 |
| UIR-STATE-008 | STATE | Replay inactive/positioned/playing/paused/evaluating/blocked/coverage limit | Explicit historical cursor and processing boundary; no new decision can leap unfinished evaluation | Pause/resolve prerequisite; no forced closure at coverage end | FR-19–FR-22/40/53; TX-002; TR-19 |
| UIR-STATE-009 | STATE | Draft/accepted waiting/effective pending/stop triggered/cancelled/filled/invalid change | Acceptance, trigger and fill are separate; rejected modification preserves previous valid order | Correct current terms or inspect result; no retroactive cancel/fill | FR-40/53; TR-21–TR-26 |
| UIR-STATE-010 | STATE | Missing historical FX/specification/session/price prerequisite | Original affected execution is blocked/uncommitted with no fill/fee/partial account effect | Compatible original-event resolution or deliberate restart; play does not bypass | FR-42/50; FAIL-010/014–FAIL-015; CONTRACT-007 |
| UIR-STATE-011 | STATE | Independent open/reduced/closed position; nonpositive balance | Targeted remainder/protection; risk sizing unavailable at nonpositive balance | Valid fixed sizing still possible without margin checks; no over-close/opposite-entry netting | FR-38/49/54; TR-27–TR-30 |
| UIR-STATE-012 | STATE | Retained run differs from revised current catalog/settings | Original selected evidence/results remain the run basis | Resume unchanged or explicitly restart with updated data; no automatic improvement by new ticks | FR-45; CM-005; CONC-007/012 |
| UIR-STATE-013 | STATE | Second writer, lost authority, stale confirmation or wrong target | Read-only/denied means no mutation, not a new application role | Fresh valid owner/intent required; retry cannot grant authority | FR-55; FAIL-021; SECR-001–SECR-002/006 |
| UIR-STATE-014 | STATE | Backup/output validating, failed/incomplete/unknown or complete; replacement preparing | Candidate is not yet active or a proven complete artifact | Validate/reconcile; preserve original active state; prevent repeat destructive operation while unknown | FAIL-016–FAIL-020/023; TX-005 |
| UIR-FEED-001 | FEED | Any accepted selection, edit, command, save, acquisition or export begins | Identify intended scope and pending work promptly; distinguish intention from completion | Same operation context persists until semantic outcome; no arbitrary completion promise | WF-001–WF-009; CM-001–CM-007 |
| UIR-FEED-002 | FEED | Trading decision accepted, fill applied or prerequisite blocks | Report accepted-after-boundary terms separately from a whole simulated execution or explained hold | Never announce an accepted order as executed; show coherent effect and save condition separately | CONTRACT-005–CONTRACT-007; TX-002 |
| UIR-FEED-003 | FEED | Long acquisition/detail/copy/reconstruction work | Report known phase, requested scope and qualified progress; retain useful history | No fabricated completion percentage, provider deadline or fill for responsiveness | FR-32/36/56; FAIL-006/018; MET-003/005 |
| UIR-FEED-004 | FEED | Cancel, obsolete result or version conflict | Distinguish cancelled preview, discarded obsolete response and current state requiring a fresh decision | Preserve valid current work; show meaningful result without replaying old destructive intent | CONC-002/005/008; RETRY-003/008 |
| UIR-FEED-005 | FEED | End of replay, output completion, saved acknowledgement or recovered state | Identify actual boundary/version/artifact and its limits, including unsaved newer work | No run deletion on exit or claim of full acquisition from a partial span | FR-22/44/56; CM-003/005; IDEM-004/005 |
| UIR-ERR-001 | ERR | Invalid settings/order/quantity/SL/risk or over-close | Explain offending field/constraint; prior valid configuration/instruction remains | Correct and make a fresh valid decision; no hidden margin rule | FR-12/38/49–FR-50/54; FAIL-001; PRE-05/06/08 |
| UIR-ERR-002 | ERR | File/read/provider request fails or conflicts | Explain affected range/side/access and what retained data remains usable | Qualified read/reconnect honoring provider limits; no empty-response closure or provider substitution | FAIL-002/004–FAIL-008; EXT-001–EXT-003 |
| UIR-ERR-003 | ERR | Missing/inapplicable historical prerequisite | Explain original point and why evidence does not qualify | Resolve compatible evidence; incompatible committed-basis change requires explicit restart | FAIL-009–FAIL-010/014; RETRY-005 |
| UIR-ERR-004 | ERR | Storage/export failure versus uncertain publication | Known failure leaves prior saved generation; unknown may already have published | Inspect exact reference/artifact before retry; never resubmit execution/edit as a save fix | FAIL-003/007/012–FAIL-013/017–FAIL-019/023; RETRY-004/007 |
| UIR-ERR-005 | ERR | Malformed/incompatible/missing-input backup or resume | Explain safe validation/integrity reason without loading partial state/latest substitutes | Reject candidate, preserve prior work; use a valid coherent backup/retained basis | FAIL-018/020; PRE-10; SECR-004/005 |
| UIR-ERR-006 | ERR | Stale authority, target/version conflict or forbidden disclosure | Explain restriction using safe scope/reason; no raw secret/path content | Fresh intent after current-state review; renewed confirmation when meaning changes | FAIL-011/016/021; SECR-001–SECR-008; RETRY-003/008 |
| UIR-SAFE-001 | SAFE | Rewind to an earlier cursor | Confirm run/destination, removal of later trading actions and dependent fills/fees/account effects; earlier actions retained and drawings unchanged | Cancel preserves cursor/run; confirm prepares coherent prefix replacement; unknown reconciles first | FR-21; BR-17; TX-005; FAIL-016–FAIL-017 |
| UIR-SAFE-002 | SAFE | Reset/delete saved work or restart replacing exercise/data basis | Confirm exact affected work, replacement/deletion and updated-input selection where applicable | No automatic branch/archive promised; cancel unchanged; stale scope requires fresh confirmation | FR-45/55; POL-09/11; SECR-002 |
| UIR-SAFE-003 | SAFE | Restore manual backup over current work | Identify included analysis/runs, validated original-input completeness and coherent replacement scope; no merge/credential restoration | Validate before activation; cancel/reject preserves work; uncertainty resolved before another replacement | FR-55; PRE-10; TX-005; FAIL-020 |
| UIR-SAFE-004 | SAFE | Historical entry/close/configuration and planned risk | Make hypothetical scope, USD/units, after-decision eligibility, independent target, fees and gap-loss possibility understandable before commitment | Reviewed entry direction/terms must match the enabled submission action; direction selection is preparation and requires no extra confirmation. No universal extra confirmation invented; invalid target/quantity cannot be legalized by confirmation | FR-27/38–FR-42/49–FR-50/54; POL-04–POL-08/12 |
| UIR-SAFE-005 | SAFE | Result/report/image/backup disclosure | Keep simulation/evidence/assumption labels and relevant basis; exclude credentials and private captured identities; backup retains included-run inputs | Reject prohibited/incomplete output; no hosted publication implied | FR-18/29/37/48/55; SEC-03–SEC-04/06; SECR-008 |

## 5. Accessibility, Environment & Continuity

### Settled upstream obligations

| ID | Category | Requirement | Scope | Provenance |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UIR-A11Y-001 | A11Y | Named controls, keyboard-operable commands/dialogs, clear focus, matching pointer/keyboard availability and readable values outside plot | All supplied baseline command/dialog/value surfaces; no contradictory disabled semantics | FR-24; AC-017 |
| UIR-A11Y-002 | A11Y | Status meaning cannot rely on color alone | Every state/error/save/feed/result distinction in default interface | FR-24 |
| UIR-ENV-001 | ENV | Desktop mouse/keyboard; maximum two simultaneous charts; mobile/touch outside v1 scope | Core chart tasks remain usable as panels resize; 1280×800 CSS-pixel workspace baseline, with accessibility adaptation below that area | FR-17/25; U12; accepted UIR-DEC-005 |
| UIR-ENV-002 | ENV | Saved local history/analysis remains readable without live connectivity; live/history acquisition requires available provider access | App runs locally with data-only network access; acquisition while running, catch-up on start/reconnect | FR-31–FR-35/43–FR-44/56; EXT-001–EXT-004 |
| UIR-ENV-003 | ENV | Full archive access independent of 100k resident bars/chart; retain responsive chart use during detailed resolution | Benchmark two charts, 100k resident bars each, four study panes and 100 drawings total on recorded hardware; ready-local viewport <2s, interaction p95 <50ms, live display within 1s of local receipt | SCALE-03, FR-36/52/56; AC-016; TF-010 |
| UIR-A11Y-003 | A11Y | Every primary task operable by keyboard, including chart inspection and drawing creation/editing through named commands and explicit financial anchors; brush offers editable points | Equivalent outcomes, no keyboard trap, meaningful focus order/restoration and persistent feedback | Accepted UIR-DEC-001; FR-24 |
| UIR-A11Y-004 | A11Y | WCAG 2.2 AA is the conformance target for the supported desktop interface | Include accessible numeric chart inspection, task alternatives, resize/zoom/reflow behavior and keyboard/focus semantics; implementation must later demonstrate conformance | Accepted UIR-DEC-002 |
| UIR-A11Y-005 | A11Y | Default light/dark themes convey meaning without hue alone and meet the accepted contrast target; no additional selectable color-vision theme required in v1 | Financial direction, selection, chart-series identity, status, warning and result evidence | Accepted UIR-DEC-003; FR-10/24 |
| UIR-A11Y-006 | A11Y | Honor system reduced-motion preference by removing decorative motion/transitions while retaining explicit playback controls and immediate state updates | Do not change historical event ordering or simulation playback semantics; no motion-only critical feedback | Accepted UIR-DEC-004 |
| UIR-CONT-001 | CONT | Preserve instrument/period/side/analysis on style switch; valid labelled chart on failed selection; financial anchors on zoom/resize | Chart context across reversible selection/configuration changes | FR-03/05–FR-06/14; TR-06 |
| UIR-CONT-002 | CONT | Preserve layout/instrument drawings across timeframe switches, trading rewind and restart; show them as user annotations | Authored analysis independent of simulated history; destructive analysis changes remain scoped | FR-14/21; INV-13; REL-04 |
| UIR-CONT-003 | CONT | Resume saved replay cursor/actions/orders/positions/settings/results using original inputs | Current-catalog corrections, new ticks and specification edits cannot change the retained run basis | FR-22/44–FR-45; CM-005 |
| UIR-CONT-004 | CONT | Keep coherent prior authoritative work through pending requests, previews, cancelled changes and prepublication failures | Candidate work does not become active success; saved and working versions remain distinct | FR-10/12/16/44; CM-001/003/006; FAIL-001–FAIL-003/016–FAIL-020 |
| UIR-CONT-005 | CONT | All replay panels/results/export use common historical boundary; preserve unavailable reasons rather than later values | Current feed may continue acquiring without revealing future prices; same cursor need not mean same observation time | FR-09/20/35/45; CONTRACT-012/020; ORDER-005 |
| UIR-CONT-006 | CONT | A retained original blocked event keeps its time, basis and reason until compatible resolution | New receipt does not authorize later-price execution or rewriting prior outcomes | FR-42/45–FR-46; HIST-08; TX-006 |
| UIR-CONT-007 | CONT | Backup/restore preserves included analysis/run input meaning under fresh local authority | No secrets, silent merge, restored command capability or reissued historical actions | FR-55; PRE-10; SECR-004–SECR-005 |

No source fixes a localization target, exact shortcut inventory, undo depth, initial selection, chart geometry or application credential-entry UI. Preserve UTC/source meaning and explicit units; resolve supported tool parameters and numerical conventions under their owning DEFER items before affected implementation. Do not infer a new product capability from that freedom.

## 6. UI Project Decisions — Human Gate G2

All five recommended choices were explicitly accepted together on 19 October 2026: “I accept the recommended Gate G2 scope”. Alternatives are retained as decision provenance, not open choices. The accepted requirements are UIR-A11Y-003–UIR-A11Y-006 and UIR-ENV-001.

| ID | Decision Needed | Why Downstream Design Cannot Safely Decide It | Recommendation & Alternatives | Project Decision |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UIR-DEC-001 | Scope of keyboard/non-pointer operation beyond baseline commands/dialogs | Chart inspection, financial anchoring, brush editing and selection need planned alternatives if all primary tasks must be operable without a pointer | **Accepted recommendation:** every primary task keyboard-operable, including selecting bars and creating/editing drawings through named commands and explicit anchors; brush has an equivalent editable point sequence. Alternative considered: upstream command/dialog scope only | ACCEPTED; UIR-A11Y-003; mouse/keyboard primary modality already settled by U12 |
| UIR-DEC-002 | Accessibility conformance target | A target determines downstream semantics, adaptation, input alternatives and verification scope; upstream FR-24 is narrower than a complete conformance commitment | **Accepted recommendation:** WCAG 2.2 AA target for the supported desktop interface, including chart value access and task alternatives. Alternative considered: baseline FR-24 only | ACCEPTED; UIR-A11Y-004; target is not a claim of achieved conformance |
| UIR-DEC-003 | Color-vision strategy: robust default versus additional selectable capability | An alternate color-vision theme is a product capability beyond default non-color status; downstream visuals must know which is required | **Accepted recommendation:** default light/dark themes carry non-color meaning and required contrast; no additional selectable color-vision theme in v1. Alternative considered: robust defaults plus selectable color-vision theme | ACCEPTED; UIR-A11Y-005; default non-color status already binding |
| UIR-DEC-004 | Reduced-motion expectation | Focus/context and chart-update presentation must remain usable without motion; replay speed and actual financial time progression have separate meaning | **Accepted recommendation:** honor system reduced-motion preference, remove decorative/animated transitions, preserve explicit playback controls and immediate updates. Alternative considered: no motion throughout | ACCEPTED; UIR-A11Y-006 |
| UIR-DEC-005 | Minimum supported desktop viewport/adaptation | Two charts, studies, accessible numeric alternatives and safety-critical trading information compete for space | **Accepted recommendation:** 1280×800 CSS-pixel workspace minimum, collapsible secondary regions and both charts reachable. Alternative considered: 1440×900. CSS dimensions are workspace area, not physical screen pixels; accessibility adaptation remains subject to UIR-DEC-002 | ACCEPTED; UIR-ENV-001; zoom/reflow must not strand primary actions or safety information; no mobile/touch expansion |

### Gate review and scope of this handoff

| Quality gate | Assessment at revision 2 |
|---|---|
| Authority/traceability | Parts I–III traced to requirements/domain/architecture and accepted G2 scope; source menus do not expand scope. ADV-VER-001 remains outstanding |
| Application operation coverage | CONTRACT-001–CONTRACT-011 covered by tasks; internal CONTRACT-012–CONTRACT-020 explicitly classified; no invented manual-fill action |
| Information/state/feedback/recovery/safety | Covered in Sections 3–5, including original-event blocks, asynchronous save uncertainty, exact destructive scope, full archive and replay isolation |
| G2 environment/accessibility | SATISFIED: UIR-DEC-001–UIR-DEC-005 explicitly accepted; translated into requirements in Section 5 |
| Flow/structure completeness | PASS for UX design: Sections 7–14 cover all tasks, contexts, navigation transitions and material view states; final review in Section 14 |
| Design boundaries | No palette/type/spacing tokens, wireframes, UI framework, routes, component library or production code selected |

# PART II: INTERACTION FLOWS, NAVIGATION LOGIC & RECOVERY MODEL

## 7. Logical Interaction Contexts (`IF-CTX-*`)

These are task contexts, not application routes. The active layout/chart, requested versus adopted basis, current-history versus retained-run mode, historical boundary, selected object/order/position and actual save condition travel with every applicable transition. A temporary interaction cannot substitute its own latest-data basis. [UIR-CONT-001–UIR-CONT-007; CONTRACT-012/020]

| Context ID | Name & purpose | Enters with | Required information | Available intents | Preserved context / sources |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IF-CTX-001 | Market exploration | Startup, restored layout or return to chart | UIR-INFO-001–UIR-INFO-006, UIR-INFO-012/013 | UIR-ACT-001/002/004/005/006/007/012 | Layout/chart/basis/viewport; FR-01–FR-09/17/20; CONTRACT-001 |
| IF-CTX-002 | Selection and observation inspection | Search, selected bar, watchlist item or coverage query | UIR-INFO-001–UIR-INFO-004 | UIR-ACT-001/002 | Search query, candidate versus adopted selection, selected time; CONTRACT-001 |
| IF-CTX-003 | Analysis and simulation configuration | Selected chart/study or named simulation settings scope | UIR-INFO-005/007/008/013 | UIR-ACT-003/008 | Draft versus committed settings, origin, run-selected versions; CONTRACT-002/011 |
| IF-CTX-004 | Annotation authoring and management | Layout/instrument, supported tool or selected object | UIR-INFO-001/005/013/014 | UIR-ACT-004 | Financial anchors, target identity, preview and undo meaning; CONTRACT-002 |
| IF-CTX-005 | Layout selection and arrangement | Current named analysis, one/two charts | UIR-INFO-001/005/013 | UIR-ACT-005/014/015 | Source layout, active chart, independent interval/sync choices; FR-16–FR-17 |
| IF-CTX-006 | Historical run progression | New usable start or selected saved run | UIR-INFO-002/006/009/011/013 | UIR-ACT-006/007/009/010/011/012 | Retained basis, common cursor, processed boundary and positions; CONTRACT-004/008 |
| IF-CTX-007 | Historical instruction preparation | Writable run at completed decision point, entry intent or selected pending order | UIR-INFO-006–UIR-INFO-009 | UIR-ACT-008/009 | Draft terms, original pending terms, applicable spec/fee version; CONTRACT-005/011 |
| IF-CTX-008 | Position and result inspection | Selected own run, order/position/execution or result family | UIR-INFO-006/009/010/013 | UIR-ACT-009/010/012 | Target identity, remainder, result selection/filter, bounded evidence; CONTRACT-005/006/010 |
| IF-CTX-009 | Original-event prerequisite resolution | Coherent paused prefix and retained original event/reason | UIR-INFO-006/008/010/011/013/015 | UIR-ACT-011/007/013/016 | Original occurrence/time/price context, no partial effects; CONTRACT-007 |
| IF-CTX-010 | Scoped destructive decision | Rewind/reset/delete/restart/restore proposal against a particular target/version | UIR-INFO-006/013/014 | UIR-ACT-007/015 | Invoker, target/version, proposed consequence, old authoritative state; CONTRACT-008/009 |
| IF-CTX-011 | Data continuity and access inspection | Runtime acquisition, no receipt, outage, gap or coverage query | UIR-INFO-001/002/012/015 | UIR-ACT-013/016 | Requested series/span, qualified catalog, separate replay basis; CONTRACT-003 |
| IF-CTX-012 | Retained work and local recovery | Saved state query, restart, writer restriction, backup/restore request | UIR-INFO-005/006/013–UIR-INFO-016 | UIR-ACT-014/015/016 | Coherent retained versions, selected analysis/runs, fresh authority; CONTRACT-009/019 |
| IF-CTX-013 | Local output preparation | Chart image or own-run report intent | UIR-INFO-001/006/010/016 | UIR-ACT-012 | Pinned source snapshot/boundary and return target; CONTRACT-010 |

### Shared traversal and timing rules

**IF-A11Y-001 — common task traversal:** Start with context name, adopted basis/mode and important restriction; reach task selection, required inputs, current values, commit/cancel and outcome in meaningful order. Chart commands have named keyboard alternatives; selected-bar numerical inspection does not require hover. Entering a temporary task moves focus to its purpose or first relevant input; leaving restores the invoking control/selection, or nearest surviving meaningful target after deletion/replacement. No keyboard trap, unexpected focus jump on refresh or timing-only critical message. Live progress is available on demand without announcing every price tick. Non-color semantics and reduced-motion behavior apply throughout. [UIR-A11Y-001–UIR-A11Y-006]

**IF-CONT-000 — return versus mutation:** Opening/closing inspection, configuration, diagnostics or output preparation does not itself submit an order, advance/rewind a cursor, restore a backup or select updated run inputs. Draft edits remain visibly distinct from committed state. Playback may continue while nonmutating detail is inspected; displays retain actual boundary labels rather than a false frozen-time promise. For a trading draft or destructive proposal, explicitly reach a paused completed decision point before showing actionable terms; if the run/target changes, invalidate/review terms and confirmation. Do not resume playback automatically on return. [CM-001/002/006; CONC-003/004/008; FR-19/40/53; UIR-SAFE-001–UIR-SAFE-004]

## 8. End-to-End User Journeys (`IF-JRN-*`)

### IF-JRN-001 — Discover and inspect complete available history

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-001; UIR-TASK-001, UIR-TASK-002, UIR-TASK-003. **Entry:** IF-CTX-001, at startup or from a valid current/replay view.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-001: inspect provider/bounds, search by supported identity and request an instrument/period/side. IF-STEP-002: load matching qualified range and adopt coherent labels/values; navigate by pan/zoom/date/preset through archive reads. IF-STEP-003: select a bar or supporting instrument and read numeric values/reference time, gaps and study availability in IF-CTX-002.
- **Commit/success:** Matching selection adopted under CONTRACT-001/TX-001; inspecting or moving the viewport alone does not move replay time. Latest-history navigation in replay is bounded by the run cursor; leaving replay is the explicit IF-JRN-006 action.
- **IF-BR-001:** No search match, unsupported period, unavailable value, unreadable interval and actual archive end differ. Crossing the resident set requests more data; higher-period values unavailable at cursor stay absent.
- **IF-FEED-001:** Show requested basis/loading at initiation, retaining old labelled data; reveal adopted range and reasoned unavailable values on completion; stale responses are discarded, not announced as selection success.
- **IF-REC-001:** Change query/basis, retry current qualified read or return to retained valid chart; absence is not zero. A retry never adopts an obsolete request. [FAIL-001–FAIL-002; RETRY-001/003]
- **IF-CONT-001 / IF-A11Y-002:** Preserve viewport anchors and query/selection on detail return; labelled keyboard pan/zoom/date/bar traversal and numeric inspection follow IF-A11Y-001. Preserve per-chart identity when switching charts.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-001/002; UIR-STATE-001/002/005; FR-01–FR-09/20/52; WF-001.

### IF-JRN-002 — Configure presentation and built-in studies

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-002; UIR-TASK-004, UIR-TASK-005. **Entry:** IF-CTX-003 from a named chart/study in IF-CTX-001.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-004: identify chart/study instance, committed settings and declared calculation/warm-up behavior. IF-STEP-005: preview supported presentation/inputs/visibility or overlay/pane placement; validate inputs. IF-STEP-006: apply valid changes or cancel, then return to that chart/study and request autosave of the committed version.
- **Commit/success:** Valid authored settings adopted under CONTRACT-002; saved is a later exact-version acknowledgement. Timezone/precision changes alter display, not source buckets/values; chart-dependent calculations use chosen bid/ask.
- **IF-BR-002:** Invalid study parameters retain committed configuration; remove targets the selected instance. Missing/warm-up output stays unavailable. Unfinished formula/input conventions remain DEFER-003, not guessed TradingView parity.
- **IF-FEED-002:** Distinguish preview, invalid field, applied working setting and actual saving outcome. Cancellation restores prior valid presentation and creates no saved preview.
- **IF-REC-002:** Correct invalid inputs or cancel; load/calculation failure retains previous valid state; retention failure uses IF-JRN-013 without replaying the edit. [FAIL-001–FAIL-003; CM-003]
- **IF-CONT-002 / IF-A11Y-003:** Preserve chart selection/range, selected study and return focus; read labels/errors before apply. Switching theme must preserve focus and non-color semantics; reduced motion never changes replay timing.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-003; UIR-STATE-005/006/007; FR-05/10–FR-12/16; WF-002; CONTRACT-018.

### IF-JRN-003 — Author and manage financially anchored drawings

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-002; UIR-TASK-006. **Entry:** IF-CTX-004 with layout/instrument and tool/object selected.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-007: choose supported tool or object and inspect ownership/lock/visibility/anchors. IF-STEP-008: create/edit preview using pointer or named numeric-anchor/point-sequence alternatives. IF-STEP-009: commit valid geometry, cancel preview, or perform selected hide/lock/delete/undo/redo, then observe saving.
- **Commit/success:** One valid authored change; cancelling an uncommitted drawing leaves no object; redo recreates no duplicate. A position-measurement drawing creates no simulated order. Delete committed work uses IF-CONF-002; undo targets supported analysis changes with a meaningful action label.
- **IF-BR-003:** Locked or missing target explains unavailable edit; showing/hiding differs from deletion. Geometry bounds and supported undo depth remain scoped realization prerequisites in Section 14.
- **IF-FEED-003:** Identify preview/commit, selected object and undo action; show validation or denied reason; separate valid working edit from saved checkpoint.
- **IF-REC-003:** Correct anchors, cancel preview, explicitly unlock a valid target, or use supported undo; late/wrong-layout result discarded; save recovery does not repeat creation. [PRE-09; TEN-003; IDEM-001]
- **IF-CONT-003 / IF-A11Y-004:** Preserve financial anchors across viewport/period changes and trading rewind. Object traversal selects by identity/type, then anchor/point values, then actions; numeric point operations provide keyboard brush equivalence. Return focus to surviving object or object collection after delete.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-004; UIR-INFO-005; FR-13–FR-15/24/55; INV-13; WF-002.

### IF-JRN-004 — Retain layouts and compare one or two charts

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-002; UIR-TASK-007. **Entry:** IF-CTX-005 from current workspace or retained work.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-010: inspect current layout identity/save condition and choose create/name/restore or arrangement. IF-STEP-011: choose one/two charts and interval-sync state; review active chart and supported interval. IF-STEP-012: apply coherent arrangement/restore, return to workspace and observe exact saved condition.
- **Commit/success:** Adopted valid layout/arrangement; synced interval changes once for both charts, without syncing instruments or sides. Selecting one chart does not authorize deletion of hidden retained authored work; any actual destructive consequence requires IF-CONF-002.
- **IF-BR-004:** Empty collection offers creation; a third chart or unsupported interval is rejected. Unsynced charts keep independent selections. Unavailable second-chart evidence remains labelled rather than copying first-chart values.
- **IF-FEED-004:** Show selected versus loaded layout, loading/invalid/retention outcome and explicit sync state. New pending selection cannot relabel prior chart content.
- **IF-REC-004:** Cancel selection, correct name/arrangement, retain previous valid workspace on load failure or use last coherent checkpoint. Do not silently restore mutable/latest run inputs. [CONTRACT-002/009; TX-001; CONC-011]
- **IF-CONT-004 / IF-A11Y-005:** Preserve layout/instrument analysis and identify active chart before scoped commands; chart switch, sync and collection operations have keyboard traversal. Restore focus to layout control or selected chart; shared replay boundary survives both arrangements.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-005; UIR-INFO-001/005/013; FR-16–FR-17/20; WF-002/008.

### IF-JRN-005 — Start, resume and advance historical replay

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-003; UIR-TASK-009. **Entry:** IF-CTX-006 via usable history/start choice or a saved run.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-013: choose new start or resume retained exercise; validate usable bounds, selected inputs/settings and actual saved point. IF-STEP-014: prepare retained evidence and position all panels at common cursor; expose play/pause/speed/step/seek with explicit step reference. IF-STEP-015: advance completed evidence, evaluate existing eligible instructions, publish coherent prefix and then enable the next decision; request autosave.
- **Commit/success:** A coherent run/cursor under CONTRACT-004; preparation alone is not a resumable saved run. Forward seek follows the same evaluations and gap checks as stepping. Step source/period must be visibly declared; exact step-control conventions remain DEFER-003 before implementation.
- **IF-BR-005:** Unresolved gap/prerequisite enters IF-CTX-009; missing originals block resume without latest substitution; actual coverage end pauses with positions retained. Range paging is not coverage end; a weekly/monthly bar cannot reveal final values early.
- **IF-FEED-005:** Identify preparation, playing/paused/evaluating, cursor, block/end and actual save condition; no new trade decision while existing-order processing is unfinished. Speed/redraw affects neither event order nor eligibility.
- **IF-REC-005:** Pause; inspect/resolve original block through IF-JRN-010; retry valid retained read or resume last saved coherent state; do not skip a gap or force-close exposure. [FAIL-009–FAIL-013; ORDER-001/005]
- **IF-CONT-005 / IF-A11Y-006:** All charts/watchlists/details/studies/results use same retained boundary with independent observation times. Keyboard start/date/step/play/pause/speed/seek and persistent cursor values follow IF-A11Y-001; critical pause reasons persist.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-006; UIR-STATE-008/010/012; FR-19–FR-23/40/45–FR-46/53; WF-004/006/008.

### IF-JRN-006 — Rewind, restart or exit a historical exercise

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-003/006; UIR-TASK-010. **Entry:** IF-CTX-006 with active/retained run and explicit operation kind.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-016: select earlier destination, same-basis restart, updated-data restart or exit. IF-STEP-017: for destructive replacement, reach a paused completed boundary and review exact target/version, discarded suffix or replacement and basis in IF-CTX-010. IF-STEP-018: confirm to prepare/reconstruct and publish whole replacement; cancel unchanged; exit directly returns to current history while retaining run.
- **Commit/success:** Rewind reconstructs retained prefix with matching position/order/fee/USD history; updated-data restart deliberately changes basis; exit is a mode transition, not deletion. Replacement success requires known authoritative publication under TX-005.
- **IF-BR-006:** Earlier target requires IF-CONF-001; reset/restart requires IF-CONF-002. Changed target invalidates old confirmation; unavailable original data prevents reconstruction. Exit identifies current-history mode and does not label unsaved suffix as saved.
- **IF-FEED-006:** Present scope before commitment, preparation while old authority remains, then known new generation or unresolved publication. Keep cancellation distinct from pending replacement.
- **IF-REC-006:** Before publication, cancel/failure leaves original state; after acknowledgement loss reconcile old/new generation before another destructive operation, then refresh all bounded views. No automatic destructive retry. [FAIL-016–FAIL-017; RETRY-004/008]
- **IF-CONT-006 / IF-A11Y-007:** Drawings remain independent; earlier entries can become open again after suffix removal. Keyboard destination/scope/confirm/cancel traversal restores run controls; cancel does not move cursor, auto-play or discard draft work.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-007; UIR-SAFE-001/002; FR-21–FR-22/45/55; WF-008; CM-006.

### IF-JRN-007 — Configure simulation meaning and planned quantity

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-004; UIR-TASK-011. **Entry:** IF-CTX-003 from named simulation scope or IF-CTX-007.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-019: inspect selected scope, fixed USD balance, instrument units/increments/minimum, metadata overrides and fee convention. IF-STEP-020: enter valid configuration and fixed/risk inputs; calculate permitted quantity/planned risk with required SL, historical FX and fees. IF-STEP-021: apply valid authorized settings to their declared scope or cancel; return to the invoking task.
- **Commit/success:** Valid simulation settings/quantity estimate under CONTRACT-011; no order is submitted. Existing run-selected versions/results do not change from editing current settings; changed committed basis routes to deliberate restart. Only permitted missing-prerequisite completion can affect original blocked event.
- **IF-BR-007:** Unknown instrument value/conversion blocks affected sizing/trading; risk mode requires positive balance and valid SL; below-minimum quantity rejects. Fixed mode needs valid instrument quantity but no margin check. Three commission modes default zero and apply on entry/every exit.
- **IF-FEED-007:** Expose units/version/override, calculated quantity/fee-inclusive planned risk or specific unavailable reason; warn that gaps can exceed planned risk. Display precision does not select monetary rounding conventions.
- **IF-REC-007:** Correct input, cancel, obtain justified missing specification or resolve applicable historical prerequisite; preserve prior settings on invalid edit. Exact monetary conventions remain DEFER-004. [PRE-04–PRE-06; CONC-012]
- **IF-CONT-007 / IF-A11Y-008:** Retain draft/order target while reviewing settings; invalidate entry validation and estimates if direction, terms, sizing inputs, basis or decision context changes; use IF-BR-008a before restoring submission availability. Keyboard fields expose units and field errors before apply; return focus to invoking sizing input.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-008; UIR-INFO-007/008; FR-39/41–FR-42/49–FR-50/54; WF-005; POL-06–POL-09.

### IF-JRN-008 — Submit, modify or cancel a historical instruction

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-004; UIR-TASK-012. **Entry:** IF-CTX-007 with writable paused run and completed decision boundary.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-022: choose new Market/Limit/Stop entry or pending order and inspect current valid terms/eligibility. IF-STEP-023: explicitly select the new-entry draft direction in the ticket, enter units/quantity/levels/SL/TP and review current direction-specific validation, size, fees, executable side and future-effective meaning; direction selection and field edits submit nothing (IF-BR-008a). IF-STEP-024: explicitly submit a new entry only with the matching replay Buy/Sell action bound to those reviewed terms, or use the separate named modify/cancel action for its existing target; receive accepted action, validation rejection, prerequisite block or reconciled original outcome; later execution uses IF-JRN-005.
- **Commit/success:** Acceptance records terms/effective boundary once under CONTRACT-005; no visible-close fill is implied. A triggered stop becomes a market instruction and is not guaranteed its trigger price. Opposite entry remains independent exposure.
- **IF-BR-008:** Invalid modification leaves valid old order; a now-filled/nonpending target cannot be cancelled retroactively. Buy/long entries use ask and sell entries bid irrespective of chart side; limit/TP bounds remain requested or better.
- **IF-BR-008a — direction and draft binding:** One new-entry draft is shared by ticket and capsule. Ticket direction controls select Buy or Sell without committing; capsule Buy/Sell remain submission actions. Only the action matching the current valid reviewed draft can be enabled; the opposite action is unavailable and any attempted activation has no effect. Direction/terms/context edits invalidate prior validation and estimates immediately; late results for an older draft cannot restore validity. See the normative transition contract in Section 14 revision 29.
- **IF-FEED-008:** Announce selected direction, validation pending/unavailable or ready, and the reason the opposite submission is unavailable; make the route to edit direction reachable without activating that disabled action. Clearly distinguish draft, submitting, accepted waiting/pending, triggered, filled and invalid request; actual fill and save feedback are separate. Do not make repeat activation create another intent while original outcome is unresolved.
- **IF-REC-008:** Correct a known invalid request; re-read/review a changed target; for lost response reconcile/retry the same logical intent in its valid context, not a newly generated duplicate. Unresolved execution prerequisite opens IF-JRN-010. [IDEM-001/002; RETRY-002/003/005]
- **IF-CONT-008 / IF-A11Y-009:** Preserve the same draft and explicit direction on ticket collapse/configuration return, but restore validity only for unchanged context. Accepted terms stay inspectable. Keyboard traversal distinguishes ticket direction selection from replay submission, exposes both selected direction and disabled reasons without hue, and keeps an enabled route to reopen the ticket. Selection retains focus and announces validation; it never moves focus to commit or submits on Enter/return from settings. Keyboard order type/fields/review/commit follow IF-A11Y-001; focus goes to erroneous field or accepted instruction outcome without starting playback.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-009; UIR-STATE-009/010; UIR-SAFE-004; FR-27/38/40–FR-42/47–FR-50/53; WF-005/006.

### IF-JRN-009 — Reduce a particular hedged position

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-004; UIR-TASK-013. **Entry:** IF-CTX-008 with selected open position and writable completed decision boundary.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-025: identify position/run/instrument/direction, remaining quantity and protection. IF-STEP-026: choose valid partial quantity or full remaining amount and review closing side, fee and after-decision effect. IF-STEP-027: submit targeted close, then inspect whole execution or original-event block after eligible progression.
- **Commit/success:** Close request acceptance differs from execution; execution reduces only target, resizes SL/TP after partial close and cancels protection after full closure. Full requested quantity applies to each eligible manual-close execution under ASM-02.
- **IF-BR-009:** Invalid/over-close rejects rather than netting or opening opposite exposure; protection or another close may change remainder before commit, requiring current-state review. Long closure sells bid; short closure buys ask.
- **IF-FEED-009:** Keep target/remainder co-present through review and acceptance; publish quantity/protection/exit commission/USD effect as a coherent result, with save condition separate.
- **IF-REC-009:** Re-read remainder/version and make a valid new decision after conflict; reconcile the same uncertain intent before repeating; missing monetary prerequisite leaves the entire original execution uncommitted. [CONC-004; TX-002; FAIL-010–FAIL-013]
- **IF-CONT-009 / IF-A11Y-010:** Preserve result filter and position selection; after closure retain historical target detail or move to a meaningful surviving collection entry. Keyboard target selection and numeric quantity alternatives require no chart drag.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-010; UIR-INFO-009/010; FR-38/41/48/54; WF-005/006; INV-05–INV-07.

### IF-JRN-010 — Resolve the original blocked historical event

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-003/004; UIR-TASK-014. **Entry:** IF-CTX-009 from progression, instruction/result inspection or resumed saved block.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-028: inspect original instrument/event/time, last coherent prefix and exact gap/session/price/FX/specification reason. IF-STEP-029: inspect relevant retained evidence and pursue supported source recovery or explicit specification correction; qualify historical applicability/compatibility. IF-STEP-030: reevaluate original occurrence once through CONTRACT-007/TX-006 when compatible, or remain paused/choose deliberate updated-data restart.
- **Commit/success:** Block resolution and the original whole execution publish together; later receipt is not a later fill price. A play command or dismissal of explanation cannot clear a prerequisite.
- **IF-BR-010:** Finer stored bars precede local ticks; unresolved eligible protective ordering alone may use labelled ASM-01. Missing price/entry/continuity cannot. New data requiring committed-basis change offers restart, never automatic mixing. No automatic missing-tick download.
- **IF-FEED-010:** Persist blocked context; distinguish evidence loading, rejected/inapplicable prerequisite, compatible reevaluation, whole applied effect and actual saving condition.
- **IF-REC-010:** Keep paused coherent state, retry qualified read, correct valid missing specification, resume later or explicitly restart. Do not invent FX, infer closure from absence or override gap by stop-first. [FAIL-009–FAIL-010/014–FAIL-015; RETRY-005]
- **IF-CONT-010 / IF-A11Y-011:** Preserve original event/context across data/settings/diagnostic visits; stale pre-rewind callback cannot unblock new epoch. Keyboard reason→evidence→permitted resolution→outcome traversal; return to original blocked result/control.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-011; UIR-INFO-011; FR-23/37/42/45–FR-46/50–FR-51; WF-007; POL-02/03/07/09.

### IF-JRN-011 — Inspect evidence and create local chart/results output

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-002/004; UIR-TASK-008, UIR-TASK-015. **Entry:** IF-CTX-008 for results, or IF-CTX-001 for chart image; IF-CTX-013 only when export requested.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-031: inspect bounded chart or recorded own-run results with units, fees, dataset/conventions and ASM labels. IF-STEP-032: reveal selected execution/study provenance as needed; choose local image or own-run report and review included source scope. IF-STEP-033: pin intended snapshot, produce local artifact and report verified completion/failure independently of source work.
- **Commit/success:** Reading results has no execution effect. Output success means a complete matching artifact; report values come from recorded bounded outcomes, not a new calculation against latest history. Own-run reports do not enable external completed-run import.
- **IF-BR-011:** Empty result history is not zero-profit evidence; unavailable metrics state their reason. Evidence distinguishes finer bars/ticks/stop-first/full-fill/bar-touch, source volume and historical conversion. Partial/unknown export is not complete.
- **IF-FEED-011:** Identify selection/preparation/output writing and actual artifact outcome. Retain source snapshot labels if workspace moves after pinning; no secret/private captured content included.
- **IF-REC-011:** Return from evidence with selected result/filter intact; failed output can retry the same pinned snapshot or be abandoned; unknown artifact verified before claiming success/repeating. Source work unchanged. [FAIL-023; IDEM-005; RETRY-007]
- **IF-CONT-011 / IF-A11Y-012:** Keep run/result selection or originating chart/range and mode/boundary; read results numerically through keyboard detail traversal. Return focus to selected execution or export invoker.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-012; UIR-SAFE-005; FR-18/20/29/37/48; WF-009; SECR-008.

### IF-JRN-012 — Understand live access and historical catch-up

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-005; UIR-TASK-016. **Entry:** IF-CTX-011 from runtime startup/reconnect or user continuity inspection.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-034: inspect actual data-only access/feed condition, receipt/event time and existing usable history. IF-STEP-035: follow requested M1/H1 recovery spans, known qualified progress, overlaps/finality and supported reconnect/read action. IF-STEP-036: inspect published complete/partial current coverage and affected aggregates; return to original chart/run.
- **Commit/success:** Qualified current-catalog publication under CONTRACT-003, not simply an HTTP response or received live tick. Final history replaces provisional current data; saved runs retain original basis. Data-only setup mechanics await verified provider integration; no credential input into browser-facing views is prescribed.
- **IF-BR-012:** No recent receipt, proven closure, stale/denied/disconnected, provisional current candle, partial recovery and unresolved conflict/gap differ. Empty response/weekend/zero volume alone prove neither closure nor invalidity. Required live access cannot be waived or replaced with demo.
- **IF-FEED-012:** Known access/progress/limitation persists, with receipt versus event time distinct; no recovery deadline/unsupported percentage. While replay active, continuity inspection may explain connection/process state but must not disclose post-cursor price observations, OHLC or later-derived metrics; full current-price inspection requires explicit exit.
- **IF-REC-012:** Preserve readable history; use qualified bounded retry/reconnect honoring provider limits; inspect unresolved spans; uncertainty in local publication reconciles before reuse. No stale callback after revocation. [FAIL-004–FAIL-008/022; EXT-002–EXT-003]
- **IF-CONT-012 / IF-A11Y-013:** Return preserves active layout/run/cursor and original inputs; do not replace replay panels with latest quotes. Keyboard access to reason/progress/retry/return, stable focus during updates, persistent status rather than tick announcements.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-013; UIR-INFO-012; FR-31–FR-35/43/56; WF-003; ORDER-005; SECR-003/007/008.

### IF-JRN-013 — Recover coherent saved work and understand authority

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-006; UIR-TASK-017, UIR-TASK-019. **Entry:** IF-CTX-012 from startup, saving/error status or denied mutation.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-037: identify current writable/read-only authority and working versus retained versions. IF-STEP-038: validate last successful checkpoint and run-input completeness or reconcile uncertain publication. IF-STEP-039: resume coherent saved work, retry known failed retention of current coherent work, or remain read-only/blocked with safe local diagnostics.
- **Commit/success:** Only exact durable retained version is saved; startup can lose saving/unsaved suffix/previews, never acknowledged coherent work. Restored historical action facts are not retransmitted commands. A lost save acknowledgement does not call for repeating a trade/edit.
- **IF-BR-013:** No prior work offers initial creation; second writer/stale authority remains denied; corrupt/missing original inputs prevent affected resume, with other usable history preserved. Lost writer requires genuinely fresh authority after exclusion, not a timeout-only takeover affordance.
- **IF-FEED-013:** Expose saving/unsaved/error/unknown and recoverable version separately; read-only reason and sanitized correlation on demand. An older saved acknowledgement cannot label newer work saved.
- **IF-REC-013:** Reconcile first if unknown; correct storage and retry retention, use last coherent checkpoint or own validated backup, close conflicting owner through supported lifecycle, or continue permitted inspection. No automatic authority escalation or command resubmission. [FAIL-003/012–FAIL-013/018/021; RETRY-004/007]
- **IF-CONT-013 / IF-A11Y-014:** Preserve known saved generation and scope; coherent recovery restores cursor/orders/positions/settings together. Keyboard saved-state/diagnostic traversal restores meaningful context; error updates never steal focus.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-014/016; UIR-INFO-013/015; FR-44–FR-45/55; WF-008; DIAG-005–DIAG-007.

### IF-JRN-014 — Back up, restore or deliberately discard local work

- **Goal/tasks:** UIR-GOAL-006; UIR-TASK-018. **Entry:** IF-CTX-012 with selected owned analysis/runs or own backup candidate.
- **Path:** IF-STEP-040: choose backup, restore, delete or reset and inspect exact included scope/required run inputs. IF-STEP-041: backup pins validated retained content; restore stages and validates integrity/compatibility; destructive action enters IF-CTX-010 for exact scope/version confirmation. IF-STEP-042: write/verify complete backup or atomically publish validated confirmed replacement/deletion, establish fresh authority and report actual outcome.
- **Commit/success:** Backup artifact is complete and credential-free; restore is coherent replacement, not conflict merge or external-run import. Delete/reset affects only confirmed owned scope. Confirmation cannot legalize malformed inputs or sibling-source writes.
- **IF-BR-014:** Unsafe/malformed/incompatible/missing-input candidate rejects before activation. Work changes after review invalidate confirmation. Backup captures the identified retained snapshot; newer unsaved work must not be claimed included. Cancellation before replacement preserves active work.
- **IF-FEED-014:** Expose selected scope, validation/copy/preparation, confirmation, known success or unresolved publication; output failure and replacement outcome differ. No automatic destructive retry.
- **IF-REC-014:** Choose valid backup/correct storage/retry same pinned output; validation failure retains old active state. Resolve uncertain publication before another replacement; recreate fresh intent/confirmation for changed scope. [FAIL-016–FAIL-020; RETRY-004/007/008]
- **IF-CONT-014 / IF-A11Y-015:** Original state stays authoritative until known replacement; new epoch fences stale callbacks and backup imports no secrets/capabilities. Keyboard scope→validation→consequence→confirm/cancel→outcome; return to origin or surviving restored work, never a removed target.
- **Authority:** UIR-ACT-015; UIR-SAFE-002/003/005; FR-55; WF-008; PRE-10; SECR-002/004–SECR-005.

## 9. Context Transitions, Confirmations & Progressive Disclosure

### Navigation (`IF-NAV-*`)

Opening a surface below is navigation only. A commit/resolution/restore action separately invokes the corresponding journey and authority contract. Every return applies IF-A11Y-001 and IF-CONT-000.

| ID | From → to contexts | Trigger | Preserved context | Reversibility / source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| IF-NAV-001 | IF-CTX-001 ↔ IF-CTX-002 | Search/bar/watchlist/coverage inspection; return | Chart/query/range/selected time and common boundary | Inspection reversible; adopting a new selection is explicit; IF-JRN-001 |
| IF-NAV-002 | IF-CTX-001/004/007/008/009 ↔ IF-CTX-003 | Configure selected analysis or simulation scope; return | Invoker, committed settings, draft/target and run-selected basis | Cancel restores prior valid values; IF-JRN-002/007 |
| IF-NAV-003 | IF-CTX-001 ↔ IF-CTX-004 | Select tool/object or leave authoring | Layout/instrument and anchors; uncommitted preview distinguished | Cancel preview leaves no object; IF-JRN-003 |
| IF-NAV-004 | IF-CTX-001 ↔ IF-CTX-005 ↔ IF-CTX-012 | Layout/arrangement selection or saved-work inspection | Current layout, active chart, source save condition | Return does not restore/delete without action; IF-JRN-004/013 |
| IF-NAV-005 | IF-CTX-001/012 → IF-CTX-006 → IF-CTX-001 | Start/resume replay; explicit exit to current history | Run retained on exit; mode labels change; source layout preserved | Return requires bounded view for actual mode; IF-JRN-005/006 |
| IF-NAV-006 | IF-CTX-006/008 ↔ IF-CTX-007 | Prepare entry or edit pending instruction | Run/decision point/draft or target identity | Return does not submit/cancel/auto-play; IF-JRN-008 |
| IF-NAV-007 | IF-CTX-006 ↔ IF-CTX-008 | Inspect positions/orders/results, prepare targeted close | Cursor/basis, result filter and target | Close request is separate commit; IF-JRN-009/011 |
| IF-NAV-008 | IF-CTX-006/007/008 ↔ IF-CTX-009 | Block explanation/resolution; return | Original event/prefix and selected target | Return does not clear prerequisite or play; IF-JRN-010 |
| IF-NAV-009 | IF-CTX-006/004/005/012 ↔ IF-CTX-010 | Destructive proposal or cancel/result return | Exact action/target/version and return origin | Cancel unchanged; confirmation publication may be irreversible without backup; IF-JRN-003/004/006/014 |
| IF-NAV-010 | IF-CTX-001/009/012 ↔ IF-CTX-011 | Coverage/access/recovery inspection; return | Requested span, source run/cursor, data disclosure restriction | Retry distinct from navigation; IF-JRN-012 |
| IF-NAV-011 | Any applicable context ↔ IF-CTX-012 | Save/owner/diagnostic/recovery query | Exact saved/working scope, safe operation context and invoker | Return does not replay command or replace work; IF-JRN-013/014 |
| IF-NAV-012 | IF-CTX-001/008 ↔ IF-CTX-013 | Local image/report preparation; return | Pinned source snapshot and invoking selection | Abandon leaves source unchanged; IF-JRN-011 |

### Confirmation (`IF-CONF-*`) and disclosure (`IF-DISC-*`)

| ID | Type | Context / action | Trigger and visible scope / secondary content | Confirm / cancel / return rule | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IF-CONF-001 | CONF | IF-CTX-010 trading rewind | Run, earlier destination, discarded later actions/dependent effects, retained prefix and untouched drawings; current version | Confirm prepares replacement; cancel unchanged at existing decision point; stale scope re-reviewed; unknown publication reconciled | UIR-SAFE-001; IF-JRN-006; TX-005 |
| IF-CONF-002 | CONF | Committed object/layout/run delete, reset, same/updated-basis restart | Exact selected work and consequence; distinguish input-basis replacement from same-basis reconstruction; no promised automatic branch | Confirm only valid scope/version, cancel unchanged; known failed preparation preserves old state; no automatic retry | UIR-SAFE-002; IF-JRN-003/004/006/014; SECR-002 |
| IF-CONF-003 | CONF | Validated own-backup restore | Included analysis/runs/input completeness, current work to replace, no merge/credential restoration | Reject invalid before confirmation/activation; cancel unchanged; known replacement under fresh authority; reconcile unknown | UIR-SAFE-003; IF-JRN-014; PRE-10 |
| IF-DISC-001 | DISC | Selection/observation inspection | Primary basis/value/absence reason stays visible; reveal coverage, metadata and source quality for selected series | Return preserves query/bar/range; no future replay value | UIR-INFO-001–UIR-INFO-004; IF-JRN-001 |
| IF-DISC-002 | DISC | Configuration/object editing | Show active settings, target, validity and apply/cancel first; reveal convention/warm-up or anchor/point details on request | Preserve valid original/draft separately; return restores invoker | UIR-INFO-005/008; IF-JRN-002/003/007 |
| IF-DISC-003 | DISC | Instruction/result inspection | Terms/target/remainder/planned risk and relevant ASM label primary; reveal full spec/fee/FX/bar/tick provenance on demand | Preserve selection/filter and original run basis; no false observed path | UIR-INFO-007–UIR-INFO-011; IF-JRN-008–IF-JRN-011 |
| IF-DISC-004 | DISC | Data continuity and blocked event | Keep original block/access reason and allowed next action visible; reveal span/lineage/session qualification and safe diagnostics | Return preserves event/cursor; connection details omit post-cursor price facts during replay | UIR-INFO-011/012/015; IF-JRN-010/012 |
| IF-DISC-005 | DISC | Saved work/owner/recovery | Keep saved/unsaved/unknown/restricted meaning visible; reveal generation/integrity/correlation and backup content detail | Diagnostics do not imply restore/owner takeover; preserve operation and origin | UIR-INFO-013/015/016; IF-JRN-013/014 |
| IF-DISC-006 | DISC | Local output preparation | Output kind/source scope/cursor and complete/failure condition primary; reveal included legend/evidence/conventions | Return to original selection or still-pinned output source; source unchanged | UIR-INFO-010/016; IF-JRN-011 |

### Journey stress review

All fourteen journeys identify entry/prerequisites, a separate commitment or read-only outcome, preserved return context, pending/failed/uncertain feedback and keyboard equivalents. Repeated activation reconciles the original intent; a changed target requires re-decision, never reuse of old destructive approval. Background progress preserves prior labelled authority; navigating to detail does not advance trading or adopt newer data. A stale draft is reviewable, not silently executable. [UIR-CONT-001–UIR-CONT-007; IDEM-001–IDEM-005; CM-001–CM-007]

# PART III: INTERFACE STRUCTURE, VIEW INVENTORY & STATE MATRIX

## 10. Context-to-View Mapping & View Inventory (`UIS-VIEW-*`)

A view is a recognizable task surface, not necessarily a separate page or a simultaneously open panel. Merge inspection with work when it needs the same basis; give configuration, recovery and destructive commitment distinct focus because they have different drafts/authority and return behavior. The workspace remains the context anchor. This specifies semantic organization without fixed geometry, component types or routes.

| View ID | Name / purpose and tasks | Hosted contexts | Grouping rationale | Entry / exit and persistent context | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UIS-VIEW-001 | Chart and replay workspace; UIR-TASK-001–UIR-TASK-003/006/009/010 | IF-CTX-001/002/004/006 | Chart navigation, numeric inspection, annotation and replay share the same instrument/financial coordinates; replay controls do not require a separate chart destination | Startup, saved layout/run or return; exits use scoped task navigation; preserve layout/chart/basis/range, run/cursor and selected object | IF-JRN-001/003/005/006; UIR-CONT-001–UIR-CONT-006 |
| UIS-VIEW-002 | Scoped configuration; UIR-TASK-004/005/011 | IF-CTX-003 | Common preview/validity/apply/cancel lifecycle; analysis settings and simulation settings remain distinctly named sub-contexts | Enter from selected chart/study/order/block; return to exact invoker; preserve committed values separately from draft | IF-JRN-002/007; UIR-STATE-006/012 |
| UIS-VIEW-003 | Layouts, retained work and recovery; UIR-TASK-007/017/018/019 | IF-CTX-005/012 | Named work, exact saved state, restore and authority are one coherent local-work family; distinguish layout selection, autosave recovery and backup tasks | Workspace/startup/save restriction entry; return to origin or restored work; retain selected work and saved/working version | IF-JRN-004/013/014; UIR-INFO-005/013/015/016 |
| UIS-VIEW-004 | Historical instructions, positions and results; UIR-TASK-012/013/015 | IF-CTX-007/008 | Terms, target exposure and outcomes need simultaneous run context; entry drafting and position closure have separate targets/commit actions | From run or selected result; return to same cursor/chart; retain draft, pending terms, result filter/target | IF-JRN-008/009/011; UIR-SAFE-004 |
| UIS-VIEW-005 | Original historical-event resolution; UIR-TASK-014 | IF-CTX-009 | Missing evidence and original-event compatibility need focused recovery distinct from editable order terms or current catalog | From blocked run/result or resumed block; return without clearing reason; retain event/time/basis/prefix | IF-JRN-010; UIR-INFO-011 |
| UIS-VIEW-006 | Data continuity and access; UIR-TASK-016 | IF-CTX-011 | Acquisition/access/session/coverage explain the current-data lifecycle without making it simulation authority | From workspace/block/recovery; return to origin; retain requested series/span and replay disclosure restriction | IF-JRN-012; UIR-INFO-002/012 |
| UIS-VIEW-007 | Scoped destructive review; consequences for UIR-TASK-006/007/010/018 | IF-CTX-010 | Shared exact-scope/version review, cancellation and uncertain replacement semantics; content names actual operation | Invoker supplies target/version; return on cancel/failure or to surviving known outcome; retain old authority until publication known | IF-CONF-001–IF-CONF-003; UIR-INFO-014 |
| UIS-VIEW-008 | Local image/report output; UIR-TASK-008/015 | IF-CTX-013 | Source pinning, disclosure and artifact outcome differ from analysis/trading commitment; own-backup generation stays with retained-work view | From workspace/result; return to original selection; preserve pinned source/boundary/kind | IF-JRN-011; UIR-INFO-016 |

## 11. Structural Navigation Surfaces (`UIS-NAV-*`)

| ID | Surface type | Realizes | Destinations served | Persistence behavior |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UIS-NAV-001 | Local/contextual selection and return | IF-NAV-001 | UIS-VIEW-001 selection/value/coverage inspection regions | Query, adopted chart, selected bar and mode persist on return |
| UIS-NAV-002 | Contextual task and return | IF-NAV-002 | UIS-VIEW-002 from workspace/trading/event surfaces | Named target, draft and version; return does not apply |
| UIS-NAV-003 | Local authoring/object selection | IF-NAV-003 | UIS-VIEW-001 annotation work and object collection | Layout/instrument anchors; commit/cancel separate |
| UIS-NAV-004 | Application-level work selection/local arrangement/return | IF-NAV-004 | UIS-VIEW-003 and workspace | Active layout/chart and saved version; selecting candidate distinct from restore |
| UIS-NAV-005 | Explicit mode entry/exit | IF-NAV-005 | UIS-VIEW-001 current-history and historical-run contexts via UIS-VIEW-003 when selecting retained run | Shared boundary and retained run; exit identifies current-history mode |
| UIS-NAV-006 | Contextual entry/edit and return | IF-NAV-006 | UIS-VIEW-004 instruction preparation | Target/run decision/draft retained, no automatic submit/play |
| UIS-NAV-007 | Peer inspection and return | IF-NAV-007 | UIS-VIEW-004 positions/orders/results | Filter/selected target, actual run boundary |
| UIS-NAV-008 | Contextual problem resolution/return | IF-NAV-008 | UIS-VIEW-005 | Original event/reason/prefix; dismissing explanation cannot clear block |
| UIS-NAV-009 | Confirmation-only review/return | IF-NAV-009 | UIS-VIEW-007 from affected view | Exact scope/version; changed target requires new review |
| UIS-NAV-010 | Contextual continuity detail/return | IF-NAV-010 | UIS-VIEW-006 | Requested series/span and historical disclosure restriction |
| UIS-NAV-011 | Persistent save/authority meaning with on-demand recovery detail/return | IF-NAV-011 | UIS-VIEW-003 from all applicable views | Exact saved/working operation scope; no owner escalation by navigation |
| UIS-NAV-012 | Contextual output/return | IF-NAV-012 | UIS-VIEW-008 | Pinned snapshot and source selection; no source mutation |

Navigation reveals an unavailable destination's reason rather than silently redirecting to a different instrument, run or provider. A disabled mutation remains explainable and keyboard-discoverable through associated context; it must not masquerade as an actionable command. No role/tenant navigation is introduced. [UIR-A11Y-001/003; UIR-STATE-013; SECR-001/006]

## 12. Per-View Structural Specifications

Every region row defines its `UIS-INFO-*` group and priority, with source `UIR-INFO-*` meaning. Every action group specifies availability and nearby information. Disclosure containers are defined in Section 13. Every view also carries the common restriction/uncertainty rules in UIS-PAT-001–UIS-PAT-005; rows below describe material view-specific changes, not a replacement backend state machine. “Terminal” means the current request cannot continue without new input/authority, not deletion of all work.

### UIS-VIEW-001 — Chart and replay workspace

**Purpose/tasks:** IF-JRN-001/003/005/006; UIR-TASK-001–UIR-TASK-003/006/009/010. **UIS-A11Y-001:** Named workspace, chart identities and study regions; reading/focus sequence context→chart selection/navigation→plot or numeric alternative→annotation/inspection→replay outcome/status. Each chart has a keyboard-reachable numeric inspector and named navigation commands; switching active chart is explicit. Financial content may use necessary two-dimensional chart interaction, with equivalent task/value access outside plot. No automatic focus change on new quotes. Preserve layout/basis/anchors, actual run cursor and selected bar separately. [IF-A11Y-001/002/004/006/007]

| Region / information group | Semantic role | Priority | Meaning | Actions | Disclosure |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UIS-REG-001 / UIS-INFO-001 | Workspace identity, selection and navigation | Primary | UIR-INFO-001/002/005/006: chart/layout, period/side, mode/cursor, bounds and active chart | UIS-ACT-001/002 | UIS-DISC-001 |
| UIS-REG-002 / UIS-INFO-002 | Financial work, study and numeric observation inspection | Primary | UIR-INFO-003/004/005: plot/values, studies, watchlist/details and selected objects, consistently bounded | UIS-ACT-001/002 | UIS-DISC-001 |
| UIS-REG-003 / UIS-INFO-003 | Historical progression, decision availability and block summary | Safety-Critical | UIR-INFO-006/009/011: explicit cursor, completed-event processing, blocked point/end and next allowed decision | UIS-ACT-003 | UIS-DISC-001 |
| UIS-REG-004 / UIS-INFO-004 | Save/feed/recovery summary and diagnostics entry | Secondary; failures become Safety-Critical | UIR-INFO-012/013/015: actual feed and durable state without conflating them | UIS-ACT-004 | UIS-DISC-001 |

| Action group | Intents / scope | Role | Availability and nearby information |
|---|---|---|---|

### UIS-VIEW-002 — Scoped configuration

**Purpose/tasks:** IF-JRN-002/007; UIR-TASK-004/005/011. **UIS-A11Y-002:** Scope/purpose→fields grouped by analysis or simulation meaning→validation/estimate→apply/cancel; labels include units and errors. Configuring a study never exposes unrelated trading terms as though required. Numeric anchors/point editing may be reached from authoring while ownership stays with workspace. Preserve invoker and committed/draft versions; restore focus per IF-A11Y-003/008.
