| UIS-STATE-060 | Source advanced/superseded after pin | Original source version/cursor remains identified; optional new preparation explicit | Finish same snapshot or start new intent; no silent latest switch; IF-REC-011 | CONTRACT-010; CM-005; SECR-008 |
| UIS-STATE-061 | Known failed/incomplete artifact | Explain local output failure and incomplete state | Verify or retry same pinned snapshot/correct storage; IF-REC-011 | FAIL-023; EXT-004 |
| UIS-STATE-062 | Artifact outcome unknown | Verification responsibility persists; no false success/repeat | Verify complete matching artifact first; IF-REC-011 | IDEM-005; RETRY-007 |
| UIS-STATE-063 | Verified complete | Matching local artifact outcome and source labels visible | Return to invoking selection; no source-state mutation; IF-REC-011 | CONTRACT-010; WF-009 |
| UIS-STATE-064 | Offline/restricted disclosure/invalid source scope | Local export may continue with valid local source; forbidden/missing data or stale scope explained | Preserve source, correct source/authority, no secret leakage/hosted fallback; IF-REC-011/013 | SECR-008; SEC-03/04/06 |

## 13. Shared Structural Patterns, Disclosure & Adaptive Structure

### Disclosure containers

These are structural container roles, not prescribed widgets. Required risk/target/block/save/confirmation meaning cannot be hidden inside optional diagnostics. Closing detail restores origin/selection and never commits an operation.

| ID | View | Realizes | Structural container and invariant |
|---|---|---|---|
| UIS-DISC-001 | UIS-VIEW-001 | IF-DISC-001/002/004/005 | Secondary selection/value/object inspection and on-demand detail; chart basis/cursor/block/save meaning stays at workspace level |
| UIS-DISC-002 | UIS-VIEW-002 | IF-DISC-002/003 | Scoped configuration surface with expandable calculation/anchor/spec detail; required input/units/validity/apply/cancel co-present |
| UIS-DISC-003 | UIS-VIEW-003 | IF-DISC-005 | Work collection with selected detail, backup validation and subordinate sanitized diagnostic region; exact saved/replacement scope always accessible |
| UIS-DISC-004 | UIS-VIEW-004 | IF-DISC-003/005 | Peer instruction/position/result sub-contexts plus selected provenance detail; run/boundary/target/remainder and relevant ASM meaning retained |
| UIS-DISC-005 | UIS-VIEW-005 | IF-DISC-003/004 | Original-event detail with expandable prerequisite evidence and diagnostic explanation; missing reason and permitted next action primary |
| UIS-DISC-006 | UIS-VIEW-006 | IF-DISC-001/004 | Continuity surface with span/lineage detail and diagnostic region; preserve replay price-disclosure restriction |
| UIS-DISC-007 | UIS-VIEW-007 | IF-CONF-001–IF-CONF-003; IF-DISC-005 | Temporary scoped review with nonoptional consequence, target/version and confirm/cancel/outcome; only additional safe diagnostics expandable |
| UIS-DISC-008 | UIS-VIEW-008 | IF-DISC-006/003 | Temporary output preparation/detail; source/kind/inclusion/labels/outcome retained while extended provenance is subordinate |

## 14. Assumptions, Gaps, Traceability & Downstream Freedom

### Decision and gap disposition

There are no new `UIR-ASM-*`, `IF-ASM-*` or `UIS-ASM-*` assumptions, unresolved G2 decisions or blocking `UIR-GAP-*`, `IF-GAP-*` or `UIS-GAP-*` contradictions. Upstream ASM-01/ASM-02 remain explicitly accepted simulation approximations and visible in UIR-INFO-010, IF-JRN-010/011 and UIS-VIEW-004/005/008. The user accepted accessibility/environment scope, not numeric/provider facts. High-level UX readiness does not authorize affected implementation agents to guess deferred conventions.

| Existing prerequisite | UX consequence and owner / downstream trigger |
|---|---|
| DEFER-001 / DM-GAP-01 | Verify actual live-account API/access/entitlements and protected setup before live acceptance; UIS-VIEW-006 exposes unverified/denied access while historical mode remains usable. No credential-entry mechanism chosen by this dossier |
| DEFER-002 / DM-GAP-02 | Establish actual per-instrument session/finality/publication/historical FX applicability before affected traversal/finalization. UIS-VIEW-005/006 preserve unresolved reason; no empty-response/weekend inference |
| DEFER-003 / DM-GAP-03 | Domain/calculation/UX refinement must declare precise study formulas/initialization/input bounds, supported drawing geometry/point constraints, range-preset mappings, output options and explicit replay step-control conventions before affected brief. IF-JRN-002/003/005/011 exposes these meanings without fabricating numeric defaults |
| DEFER-004 / DM-GAP-04 | Monetary owner establishes deterministic USD/notional/quantity rounding, valuation conventions and independently justified examples before trading-calculation work. Configuration/results show selected conventions; UX does not choose multipliers/FX rates or price increments |
| DEFER-005 / DM-GAP-05 | G2 scope and high-level flow/structure resolved here; exact shortcut inventory, undo depth, initial symbol/period/watchlist order and bounded control defaults remain scoped UX/UI realization before affected interaction brief. No optional template/watchlist expansion is implied |
| DEFER-006 / VGAP-001 | Standards/integration/performance evidence selects bounded staleness/retry/diagnostic/scheduler configuration; UI exposes reasons/phases without invented timeout/attempt/SLO values |
| DEFER-007 | Standards choose stack/renderer/process/storage and prove physical durability/sole-writer capability before foundation; visual/screen skills specify later appearance/geometry. This UX structure selects none of them |
| ADV-VER-001 | Persistence verification owner must cover catalog publication racing with autosave/scoped replacement and preserve unrelated acknowledged state; this dossier keeps honest separate scopes but neither changes architecture nor discharges that proof |

### Quality-gate review

| Gate | Design assessment / evidence |
|---|---|
| Authority/traceability | PASS: Section 0 authority; every requirement/task/context/journey/view links to source semantics or explicit G2 acceptance. No captured screen/menu is promoted to feature authority |
| Interface requirements and operations | PASS: Section 2 covers nineteen tasks and CONTRACT-001–CONTRACT-011; internal contracts classified. Section 3–5 supplies information, intents, state, feedback, error, safety, accessibility and continuity |
| G2 | SATISFIED: five accepted decisions recorded verbatim with provenance and translated into UIR-A11Y/ENV obligations; no unaccepted recommendation used |
| Flow completeness | PASS: fourteen journeys cover every task, with entry, three named steps, conditional branches, commit/outcome, feedback, recovery and keyboard/context preservation; twelve transitions, three confirmation scopes and six disclosure rules |
| Truthfulness/repeat/failure | PASS as design: accepted versus executed versus saved, retained versus current basis, original-event block, qualified closure/gap, uncertain publication and same-intent retry remain distinct across relevant flows/views; physical behavior unverified |
| Structural coverage | PASS: thirteen contexts mapped to eight views with rationale, twelve transitions realized by twelve navigation surfaces; each view has regions/priorities/action groups, disclosure and eight material state rows, plus shared rules |
| Adaptation/accessibility | PASS as UX specification: per-view landmark/reading/focus rules; primary-task keyboard/numeric/anchor/point alternatives; collapsible secondary regions, two-chart access, desktop zoom/reflow and non-color/reduced-motion obligations. No conformance result claimed |
| Design-boundary restraint | PASS: no font, palette, spacing/elevation tokens, icon style, wireframe geometry, prototype, UI verification catalog, frontend framework, library, routes or production code selected |
| Remaining scope | Nonblocking for UX architecture: existing DEFER items and ADV-VER-001 retain owners/triggers above. Parts IV–VI remain pending; no G4/G5 approval inferred |

**Downstream handoff:** `READY_FOR_VISUAL_OR_WIREFRAME`. Stage 2B is now complete in visual-system.md revision 2 after explicit acceptance of all six visual directions on 19 October 2026. Proceed to `ui-screen-designer` with this view inventory, semantic hierarchy, states, accepted accessibility/environment scope and canonical visual system for low-fidelity wireframes/G4, followed by high-fidelity artifacts/verification/G5. This status is readiness for those design stages, not permission to skip them or start an affected implementation slice without its prerequisite refinements.

Visual design may select typography, palette, contrast realization, density, spacing, surfaces, iconography and reduced-motion-compatible presentation within accepted scope. Wireframe design may choose geometry, placement and concrete disclosure controls while keeping defined view boundaries, primary-task reachability, context preservation and safety-critical co-presence. Changes that alter product/domain/architecture semantics return to the owning upstream authority; changes to accepted G2 scope require explicit project revision. No separate `interface-structure.md` is created: this canonical Part III supplies that information for downstream consumers.

### Revision 17 UX delta — 21 October 2026

This scoped `ux-architecture-designer` delta resolves UIADV-INT-001 and records the user's explicit request for a settings tab locating the Dukascopy files on disk. Parts I–III below remain authoritative except for these explicit additions/refinements. G2 modality, accessibility, desktop and color-vision choices remain satisfied. No financial formula or provider behavior changes.

| ID | User-facing requirement / information / action | Authority |
|---|---|---|
| UIR-TASK-020 | Identify and configure the local source-data directory; distinguish the committed folder from a candidate and its validation outcome | Direct user request, 21 October 2026; DATA-01/16; INT-01 |
| UIR-INFO-017 | Committed and candidate absolute folder paths; readability and supported-data discovery; affected coverage/limitations; known application/save outcome | UIR-TASK-020; CM-003; supplied originals remain unchanged |
| UIR-ACT-017 | Choose or type a folder, validate the candidate, apply a valid selection or cancel without changing the committed location | Direct user request; scoped configuration intent |
| UIR-INFO-018 | Active chart calculation side independently from visible candle sides; quote-labelled OHLCV for both visible sides; study basis independently declared; both-selected displays both side-specific volume series | FR-04; DC-03; CONTRACT-001/018; UIADV-INT-001 |

**IF-JRN-015 / IF-CTX-014 — Locate local history.** Enter from the existing Settings action into UIS-VIEW-002, then select **Data location**. IF-STEP-015a: read the committed folder and type an absolute candidate path or choose Browse folder. IF-STEP-015b: Check folder reports checking, unreadable/not found, or supported assets/periods and actual coverage limitations; a readable partial dataset is qualified, never called complete. IF-STEP-015c: Apply folder commits only a validated matching candidate, reports working versus saved state and returns focus to the invoker on Close. IF-BR-015: empty/unreadable/incompatible candidates disable Apply with a reason; changing the candidate supersedes its validation; checking remains pending; Cancel retains the old location; lost writer authority denies mutation. IF-REC-015: correct path/recheck, cancel, or repair retention without applying twice (UIS-STATE-015). An inaccessible committed directory explains missing history and offers this settings entry, preserving other usable work. Candidate discovery does not overwrite source files, repin saved-run inputs or make catalog adoption/replay changes implicitly. Any affected catalog/basis change obeys existing selection/restart rules. Exact preference ownership/publication contract is an implementation prerequisite for the architecture owner, not selected here.

**IF-NAV-013 / UIS-NAV-013.** Existing Settings opens UIS-VIEW-002; Data location is an application-scoped tab within that surface, never chart-scoped simulation terms. UIR-INFO-017 occupies UIS-REG-005–007; UIS-ACT-021 carries UIR-ACT-017. Tab traversal, path editing, Browse, Check, validation message, Cancel and Apply are keyboard reachable with visible focus and status announcement; Close returns focus to Settings. Long paths wrap or remain fully inspectable. Existing desktop-zoom scrolling retains committed/candidate distinction and persistent Cancel/Apply. Use UIS-STATE-009 (valid), 010 (checking), 012 (invalid), 015 (applied/save outcome) and 016 (restricted) rather than defining a new product lifecycle. WF-099–102 realize the principal branches; applied save failure/restriction reuse 015/016 semantics. UIR-TASK-020 → IF-JRN-015 → UIS-VIEW-002.

**IF-JRN-001/002 refinement — quote calculation basis.** Settings → Symbol → Calculation basis exposes a Bid/Ask draft for the active chart. Apply requests matching observations and study/volume calculations; old content retains its old basis label until the matching result is adopted. Missing Ask data shows unavailable for that named basis, never substituted Bid or zero. Cancel preserves the committed side. Each chart selects its own basis; interval synchronization does not silently synchronize side or visibility. No basis edit changes historical execution-side rules or saved-run original inputs. This completes the already required FR-04 selection; it adds no price policy.

**UIS-PAT-012 — separate readbacks.** Toolbar Bid/Ask buttons control only candle visibility. Chart heading and numeric inspector explicitly read **Calculation basis: Bid/Ask**; OHLC, chart-dependent studies and provider-qualified volume name that side. Bid-only, Ask-only, both and neither preserve the independently selected basis and replay boundary. When neither candle side is visible, axes, drawings, study/volume outputs and numeric inspection remain; absence means hidden candles, not missing data. User refinement, 21 October 2026: when both toggles are selected, both Bid and Ask candles appear side by side at each UTC bucket; separate Bid and Ask OHLCV readouts and corresponding paired volume bars are shown. Bid candles and volume bars are filled, Ask candles and volume bars hollow. Volume values retain their side and provider qualifier; chart-dependent studies continue to name the independently selected calculation side, without inventing duplicate studies. WF-103–106 demonstrate Ask-visible/Bid basis, both/Bid, neither/Bid and explicitly adopted Ask basis. Applying basis is separate from toggling either series. These scoped UX gates pass as specification: sourced intent, complete entry/commit/recovery, unchanged G2, existing view/region ownership and no visual/framework choices.

### Revision 29 UX delta — UIADV-INT-002 — 22 October 2026

**Disposition and authority.** `READY_FOR_VISUAL_OR_WIREFRAME`: UX-owner correction supplied; independent finding closure is not asserted. This section governs the scoped entry descendants even where preserved Parts IV–VI describe their earlier approval or leave INT-002 unresolved. Sources: UIR-INFO-007/008, UIR-ACT-008/009, UIR-SAFE-004, IF-CONT-000; requirements FR-27/38–FR-42/47/49–FR-50/53–FR-54; domain PRE-03–PRE-06/PRE-08, POL-04–POL-08; architecture CONTRACT-005/011, CONC-003/012, IDEM-001, RETRY-002/003. G2/G3 scope, execution policies and backend contracts are unchanged. The existing explicit direction input and replay submission roles suffice; no new project-level accessibility/environment decision or domain rule is needed.

**Selected interaction rule (IF-BR-008a).** Keep preparation and commitment separate. The ticket owns explicit direction selection for one shared new-entry draft. Ticket Buy/Sell direction controls are labelled as selection, never execution. Capsule Buy/Sell are always submission actions: with a valid Buy draft only Buy can submit; with a valid Sell draft only Sell can submit. The opposite capsule action is disabled, conveys the reason and has no effect by pointer, keyboard or shortcut. It neither flips direction nor creates a separate shortcut intent. This preserves UIS-PAT-010's user-directed final replay commitment and avoids an action whose meaning changes between selection and submission. It adds no universal confirmation step. A control that changes direction and submits in the same activation, hidden independent side drafts and reuse of opposite-side validation are prohibited.

**Draft and validation identity.** Ticket and capsule present the same run, instrument, completed decision boundary, direction, type, sizing mode, quantity or risk input, required entry level, optional SL/TP, selected specification/fees/conversion basis and current validation/estimate result. These are semantic consistency obligations, not a new transport schema or storage model. Any relevant edit, including capsule quantity/unit editing, invalidates prior entry validity and dependent quantity, planned risk, fee and executable-side estimates before submission can run. Direction changes retain entered type, sizing inputs and price levels as editable proposed values, visibly awaiting validation; do not silently mirror levels, invent replacement prices or carry forward a valid Buy estimate as Sell. Fixed quantity still requires current permitted-quantity/specification checks; risk sizing recomputes using the selected direction and valid SL, fees, increments, minimum, positive balance and applicable historical evidence under existing rules. No new numerical SL/TP/entry formula is selected here. Missing/invalid inputs show their specific reason. Switching back to the previous direction also validates the current draft/context; an older result is not proof of current validity.

| Trigger / prior condition | Draft, readback and available actions | Commit / continuity |
|---|---|---|
| Valid reviewed Buy draft; opposite Sell attempted on capsule | Buy enabled subject to current run/authority; Sell unavailable with reason: direction is Buy, edit direction in ticket | No mutation, direction switch or command from Sell; Buy remains the only possible entry submission |
| Valid reviewed Sell draft; opposite Buy attempted on capsule | Sell enabled under the same conditions; Buy unavailable with symmetric reason and ticket route | No mutation, switch or command from Buy; Sell remains the only possible entry submission |
| Explicit ticket direction selection, or relevant terms/quantity/context change | Immediately show the selected draft direction and pending/unavailable validation; retain raw inputs for correction; both capsule submissions unavailable | Selection/typing/reopening/collapse produces no accepted instruction, cursor movement or auto-play; previous accepted orders remain intact |
| Fresh validation returns for the exact current draft/context | Current selected direction, executable side (Buy ask / Sell bid), type, levels, units/permitted quantity, fees/planned risk and after-boundary meaning are reviewable; only matching action can become available | Validation completion is preparation only; never queue a submission from an earlier click or auto-focus/activate commit |
| New direction makes retained levels invalid, SL missing, estimate unavailable or prerequisite unresolved | Explain fields/prerequisite; estimates unavailable or explicitly superseded; both submissions remain unavailable; correction and Cancel remain reachable | No automatic adjustment, margin rule or implicit order; accepted pending-order terms are unaffected |
| Matching capsule action explicitly activated | Bind the exact reviewed current direction/terms/run/boundary; recheck validity/authority at activation. If changed, show stale/invalid and require current review plus a fresh activation | Submit once under CONTRACT-005; acceptance is future-effective, not fill or save. After activation freeze the submitted terms; block both entries and direction edits that could obscure unresolved original intent |
| Submission response pending/unknown | Original request and submitted direction/terms stay visible separately from any later preparation; reconcile the same logical occurrence | No fresh opposite command or payload change under the old identity; preserve IDEM-001/RETRY-002/003. Known rejection permits correction and fresh explicit submit; acceptance stays inspectable and requires deliberate new-entry preparation before another entry |
| Ticket collapse/reopen or settings return | Preserve explicit direction and proposed inputs; shared capsule retains direction/terms summary and reachable Edit entry / direction route. Unchanged valid context may stay ready; changed scope, configuration or boundary invalidates it | Closing/reopening submits nothing; settings Apply is not an order. Cancel settings preserves entry inputs, but cannot restore validity for a changed context. Cancel entry abandons the unsubmitted draft and disables both submissions; no accepted order is cancelled |

**Co-presence and access.** UIS-REG-011/012 and UIS-ACT-010 expose the shared draft direction, validity and matching commit with current run/boundary and reviewable terms. The selected direction and disabled reason survive ticket collapse and UIS-ADAPT-004 zoom/reflow. An enabled named route opens the existing ticket at its direction editor, preserving context and restoring the invoker on close; this is an existing entry-task disclosure, not a new view. Keyboard users can select either direction, read validation/errors/terms, then reach the distinct matching replay submission. Selection keeps focus and announces new direction/validation status; disabled reasons remain readable without focusing or clicking an unavailable action. No implicit form submission, double-click shortcut or color-only side distinction can bypass the sequence. Pending-order modification/cancellation and selected-position partial/full closure retain their own targets and commit controls. Opposite entries continue to create independent hedged exposure, never close/net a selected position.

**Downstream impact and handoff.** Visual source bytes were inspected, not regenerated. Six capsule consumers currently enable both entry actions: WF-025/068/090/093/094/095. Their entry ticket/capsule availability and readback must change. WF-096 already disables both, but its Buy-only error/readback and absent direction-change branch need the same explicit editor/invalidity contract. These seven compositions, their paired-theme mockups and UI-ART entries are `STALE_VISUAL_ARTIFACT` relative to revision 29. This is semantic staleness; hashes still legitimately match the old approvals. Historical source/approval/index records are preserved and cannot authorize the revised behavior.

| Actual descendant / authority | Required owning-stage work | Approval / evidence impact |
|---|---|---|
| WF-025/068/090/093–096; WF-DEC-005/013/019; WF-ANN-004 | ui-screen-designer LOW_FI_WIREFRAMES REVISE: direction selector versus submission, matching-only availability, disabled reason and reachable editor; represent ready Sell and direction-changed pending/invalid states, including retained side-incompatible levels, fixed/risk and zoom/collapsed ticket. Choose geometry and added frame IDs in Stage 2C | Scoped G4 invalidated for these seven compositions; obtain approval of revised sources and any added material variants |
| Corresponding MP-025/068/090/093–096, both themes (14 current SVGs), UI-ART-025/068/090/093–096, UID-004 | After G4, ui-screen-designer HIGH_FI_AND_VERIFICATION: propagate actual revised sources, direction/validation/readback and state availability; update affected registry, crops/product previews, PNGs, galleries, indexes and revision/check records | Scoped G5 invalidated; preserve historical approval files and freeze new exact identities only through owning stages |
| UIV-JRN-007/008, UIV-INT-001, UIV-ADAPT-001, relevant accessibility obligations and Section 23 UI-IMPL-006 | Stage 2D owner must update observable acceptance obligations and handoff against IF-BR-008a, including symmetric wrong-side no-effect, changed-direction invalidation, stale result/activation rejection, selected-side fresh submit and no duplicate during uncertainty | Existing affected UIV/implementation evidence is stale for the revised contract; this UX delta does not author a replacement verification catalog or claim execution evidence |
| Remaining 99 current WF compositions / 198 paired-theme SVGs | Retain byte identities and approval. Shared capsule scan finds both entry actions unavailable in remaining consumers (or absent in 088); recovery, pending submission, event evaluation, modify/close and unrelated panels keep their restrictions | No blanket G4/G5 invalidation. Settings 009–016 already separate configuration from commitment; IF-CONT-007 supplies return invalidation without requiring those visuals to change |

The Stage 2C owner must trace any newly changed shared rendering code to its actual outputs before widening visual scope; do not let regeneration modify unrelated approved visuals. Preserve visual-system revision 7, independent ui-review.md revision 3 and its evidence manifest, source requirements/domain/architecture and G6 standards approvals. Data-location adoption and DEFER/ADV-VER prerequisites remain separate. Affected entry implementation remains blocked until source realization, relevant G4/G5 reapproval, verification/handoff propagation and independent recheck; unaffected foundation governance remains available.

**Scoped quality-gate assessment.** Authority/traceability: the correction specializes explicit valid-term review and semantic commands without changing financial rules. G2: existing accepted keyboard, WCAG target, non-color, reduced-motion and desktop/zoom scope retained; no unresolved project-level choice introduced. Flow: both directions now have explicit selection, invalidation, pending/error, review, commitment, rejection/uncertainty and cancellation/return behavior. Structure: existing views/regions/action groups and UIS-STATE-025/027/029/031 cover the changed branches; no new capability or destination. Design restraint: no tokens, geometry, framework, wireframes, prototypes or UIV catalog authored. This is a design consistency assessment, not independent review or runtime conformance.

**Artifact-check limitation.** The maintained wireframe checker passes for the preserved 106 SVGs. The current mockup checker reaches its all-approved dossier-status assertion and rejects revision 29 because it requires G4/G5 satisfied text; it does not model upstream semantic staleness without SVG hash drift. Its historical revision-28 authority check passes for the unchanged 212 SVGs and rebuilt viewer links. Neither result approves revision 29 visuals. Check outputs are temporary under /tmp; historical distributed reports remain unchanged. Stage 2C/2D must update the affected lifecycle/check records when realizing this delta. Python compilation and viewer regeneration pass; local links and unchanged artifact identities are checked separately.

**Next skill:** `ui-screen-designer LOW_FI_WIREFRAMES` / `REVISE`, resolve the visual propagation of UIADV-INT-002 using revision 29. Emit `READY_FOR_HUMAN_WIREFRAME_REVIEW` after actual revised artifacts and checks; the present UX handoff is `READY_FOR_VISUAL_OR_WIREFRAME` and is not G4 approval.

# PART IV: LOW-FIDELITY WIREFRAMES (`ui-screen-designer` — Stage 2C / Gate G4)

## 15. Wireframe Policy & Frame Inventory (`WF-FRAME-*`, `WF-GROUP-*`)

- **Wireframe Set Version:** `WF-SET-001-r14` — 113 frames; 025/068/090/093–096 revised, 107–113 added. Other 99 source SVGs retain exact r13 approval identities. Upstream Parts I–III revision 29 and visual-system revision 7 are preserved.
- **Canonical format:** 113 self-contained SVGs; [offline review gallery](ui-artifacts/wireframes/index.html); [frame index](ui-artifacts/wireframes/frame-index.json). Existing IDs 001–083 retain their task/state associations; geometry is revised throughout. 084 adds replay controls in the chart shell; 085 shows the chart with its right drawer closed; 086 shows the Date tab of Go to; 087 shows the Custom range tab. 088 adds start-bar selection, 089 playback, 090 paper order entry, 091 accepted waiting and 092 event processing.
- **PNG review previews:** [inline replay review](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-049.png), [Results export](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-057.png), [Go to date](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-086.png), [custom range](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-087.png), [workspace](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-001.png), [settings](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-075.png), [trading](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-025.png). These Inkscape exports are review aids; SVGs are canonical.
- **Reference-led geometry:** inspected [E01-load-chart.png](screenshots/E01-load-chart.png), [E07-settings-ready.png](screenshots/E07-settings-ready.png) and [A24-trades-state.png](screenshots/authenticated/A24-trades-state.png). E01 supplies the left tool strip, top chart toolbar, central chart, right watchlist/details and separate far-right launch rail. E07 supplies floating settings with category navigation, scrollable fields and persistent cancel/apply footer. A24 demonstrates docked results under a retained chart; no scripting/strategy capability is adopted.
- **Binding human direction:** UIS-PAT-006–009, explicitly requested/refined 20 October 2026. Settings, Go to date/range and the replay capsule may overlay. Every other task stays in reserved right drawers, bottom docks or inline controls, with the central plot resized rather than covered. The previous three-column task layout is rejected. Screenshots supply geometry, not extra product capabilities.
- **Review canvas:** baseline product surface is 1280×800 CSS pixels, excluding review margins. WF-FRAME-090 uses A32’s 1870×884 reference canvas for direct comparison, not a new minimum. Frames 065–072 show a non-normative 640×900 effective desktop zoom canvas: toolbar wraps, secondary drawers collapse, settings, Go to and replay controls may overlay; other tasks reserve space below/beside the plot and scroll within their own dock/drawer. This is no mobile mandate, minimum viewport or prescribed breakpoint.
- **Gate G4:** `APPROVED_FOR_HIGH_FI_DESIGN` — WF-SET-001-r14. User, 22 October 2026: “I approve all gate G4 wireframes”. [Current exact-hash approval](ui-artifacts/wireframes/approval-r14.json) covers all 113 sources, including seven revised and seven added frames. [Historical approval](ui-artifacts/wireframes/approval-r13.json) and [revision record](ui-artifacts/wireframes/revision.json) preserve provenance. No SVG byte changed during finalization.
- **Sample/fidelity:** EURUSD/M1/Bid is illustrative, not a startup default. Bracketed values and schematic candles/volume carry no actual financial data. Low-fidelity grayscale geometry retains approved type/density/spacing meanings; actual palette/focus styling remains Stage 2D. Replay marks stop at the displayed cursor; panels and exports retain their bounded basis.
- **Date/range refinement:** every chart now has the E01 bottom-left range strip and calendar icon, separate from the top candle-timeframe control. [A29-range-selection.png](screenshots/authenticated/A29-range-selection.png) directly supports the Go to Date tab and the presence of Custom range. Frame 087’s From/To tab body is an explicit design realization of that visible tab, not a claim that its contents were captured. Calendar month/day selection is illustrative. Preset formulas, date-zone boundary conventions and exact shortcut inventory remain under their existing DEFER owners; no Alt+G shortcut is prescribed from the screenshot.
- **Why replay confirmation / report output exist:** these are conditional actions, not primary standalone windows. FR-21/BR-17 requires confirmation before a replay rewind removes later trading decisions and recomputes positions/results; drawings stay unchanged. FR-29 and existing UIR-TASK-015/CONTRACT-010 cover exporting the manual simulation’s trades/fees/USD results and provenance. It is not a scripted strategy report or the deferred external run-bundle viewer. Rewind review is embedded in replay/work; report export is embedded in Results and appears only when invoked with the relevant context.
- **Replay / paper screenshot references:** [A30-replay.png](screenshots/authenticated/A30-replay.png), [A31-replay.png](screenshots/authenticated/A31-replay.png), [A32-paper-trading.png](screenshots/authenticated/A32-paper-trading.png) are registered as UI-REF-001/002/003 in [reference-screenshots.json](ui-artifacts/reference-screenshots.json), with original pixel digests, dimensions and explicit unknown capture provenance. The filenames do **not** amend the older controlled A30 (limit/stop), A31 (DOM) or A32 (account/cleanup) scenarios. The offline screenshot gallery now includes the three supplemental references separately; controlled activity counts remain unchanged.
- **Replay realization:** A30 supplies start-bar selection and lower transport placement. A31 supplies the actual compact floating playback/step/speed and Sell/quantity/Buy capsule. The prior decision to dock it was rejected; r7 restores floating placement over the lower chart as explicitly requested. A32 supplies right-hand paper-account/order terms and the instruction to use the replay strip. No new broker connection, order execution result or provider integration is claimed by a screenshot.
- **Paper scope:** adapt the right ticket to the approved instrument catalog, simulated USD account, Units/Risk % sizing, Market/Limit/Stop and optional SL/TP. BTC/crypto catalog entries, ATR/tick/% price order modes, equity sizing variants beyond approved risk sizing, custom studies/strategy behavior, presets, kill switch and broker-specific rules visible in the references do not become features. Monetary/quantity defaults and formulas remain under existing upstream authority. Paper account, P/L, watchlist and reports use the retained run cursor, not latest/live prices. Acceptance is never displayed as a fill or durable-save acknowledgement.
- **Dedicated replay review previews:** [select start](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-088.png), [playback](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-089.png), [paper ticket / replay strip](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-090.png), [accepted waiting](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-091.png), [processing](ui-artifacts/wireframes/previews/WF-FRAME-092.png). These remain static low-fidelity design artifacts, not an implemented replay/trading engine.
- **Future run bundles:** only a disabled, named launcher labelled future/later. No viewer content, completed-run import, export format or interaction flow is designed or enabled. Existing saved local runs and own-backup recovery remain their distinct V1 tasks.
- **State policy:** 001–064 keep matching UIS-STATE IDs. Structural changes are rendered within the persistent shell; compound-row copy/recovery obligations remain WF-ANN-001–008. The chart persists during failures/uncertainty; missing observations use reasoned absence rather than replacement application pages or fabricated prices.
- **Upstream/reproduction:** accepted G2 and visual-system revision 2 tokens remain authoritative. DEFER-001–007 and ADV-VER-001 retain their owners/triggers. Run `python analysis/tradingview/build_wireframes.py`; only local authored SVG/HTML is generated, with no target HTML execution or production code.

## 16. Wireframe Composition Annotations (`WF-ANN-*`), Assumptions (`WF-ASM-*`) & Decisions (`WF-DEC-*`)

### Composition decisions

| ID | Frames | Composition and preserved meaning | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|
| WF-DEC-001 | All frames | Chart stays mounted and visibly central. Left drawing strip and right launch rail persist; top toolbar owns manual ticker, timeframe, candles/line, indicators, replay, arrangement and settings. Right rail is not the expanded panel itself. | UIS-PAT-006; E01-load-chart.png; UIS-NAV-001–012 |
| WF-DEC-002 | 001–008/065/073/074/082–085 | Default shell shows watchlist and instrument details on the right. 085 closes that drawer to enlarge the plot. 084 now uses the compact floating capsule over the lower chart, not a reserved dock. Run identity/cursor remains in the chart heading. 088 selects the start candidate; 089 shows playing. Starting another point on an existing traded run preserves the current state until inline rewind/restart review. 073 keeps two charts inside the central work area. Drawing selection uses the left strip; numeric anchors/brush points open in an Objects drawer. | UIS-REG-001–004; UIS-ADAPT-002; VIS-DATA-001–006 |
| WF-DEC-003 | 009–016/066/075 | Settings float above the retained chart; category navigation left, scoped fields right, internally scrolling body and persistent reason/cancel/apply footer. 075 covers chart/study settings without unrelated simulation terms; simulation scope stays separately named. | E07-settings-ready.png; UIS-REG-005–007; UIS-DISC-002 |
| WF-DEC-004 | 017–024/067 | Layouts, saved runs, recovery and own-backup selection stay in a reserved local-work drawer beside the chart (a bottom dock at zoom). Candidate selection is not activation. The working/retained checkpoint, validation and authority meanings remain scoped to the selected work. | UIS-REG-008–010; IF-JRN-004/013/014; UIS-PAT-006 |
| WF-DEC-005 | 025–032/068/076 | Trading opens a right-hand entry/target drawer, with positions/orders/results in an expandable dock below the central plot, collapsed by default in paper-entry frames 025/090. Run/cursor remains above the chart and next to trade terms. Floating replay-bar paper shortcuts and the right ticket share that completed decision. The ticket prepares terms; Sell/Buy on the replay strip submits a new entry. A32 supplies grouping, while Units/Risk %, instruments and accounting follow project rules. Targeted close and pending-order modification keep distinct controls and identities. The dock may collapse without discarding results or chart state. | A24-trades-state.png; UIS-REG-011–014; UIR-SAFE-004 |
| WF-DEC-006 | 033–048/069/070/077 | Original-event resolution and data continuity open contextual right drawers. Original event/missing reason and compatible resolution stay together. Operational data status cannot expose later replay prices. Closing the drawer does not clear a block, change retained inputs or start playback. | UIS-REG-015–020; IF-JRN-010/012; UIS-PAT-002/005/006 |

### State annotations and compound-row branches

Each annotation applies to the matching WF-GROUP, including its material-state/adaptive/disclosure frames. Exact per-frame structural changes and allowed recovery are also printed outside each SVG product surface and stored in frame-index.json. Additional copy variants below must preserve geometry and scope; a future structural change requires revision before G4 finalization.

| ID | Frames / scope | Required branch treatment | Sources |
|---|---|---|---|
| WF-ANN-001 | WF-GROUP-001 / UIS-VIEW-001 | Current history and replay are explicit modes (001/082 versus 084); replay playback/order controls are docked below the plot and above range presets; the top line only identifies run/mode/cursor. Loading keeps old arrays under old labels; an absent range, no search match, warm-up and actual archive end have different reasons (003/083). End never liquidates positions. Every chart retains bottom range controls; 086/087 open floating date/range navigation above the chart without changing replay time. Invalid or beyond-boundary requests preserve prior valid viewport/selection and show a reason. Stale/offline/qualified closure has scoped evidence/time; no fabricated movement. Evaluating/slow-panel values are unavailable under the common boundary. Drawing preview, locked and hidden states preserve object identity; numerical anchors/brush points are available (074). Unknown save reconciles; known failed save retries retention, never the trade/edit. Read-only denies mutation but preserves inspection. | UIS-STATE-001–008; UIR-INFO-001–006/012/013; VIS-STATE-001–015 as applicable |
| WF-ANN-002 | WF-GROUP-002 / UIS-VIEW-002 | Calculating versus draft preview is labelled temporary. Missing spec/FX, warm-up and invalid fields have explicit absence/error; no numeric zero substitute. Changed scope makes an old estimate nonactionable. Offline local-valid settings remain usable; required missing metadata blocks only the affected apply. Applied working settings and save-failed/unknown checkpoint are separate outcomes; Cancel draft remains available during invalidity. | UIS-STATE-009–016; UIR-INFO-005/007/008/013; VIS-STATE-001–015 as applicable |
| WF-ANN-003 | WF-GROUP-003 / UIS-VIEW-003 | Empty layouts/runs state no checkpoint. Copy/validation/saving identifies phase and preserves old generation. Unsaved suffix is excluded from a pinned older backup. Known storage/corrupt-input failure blocks affected resume; invalid own-backup candidate rejects entirely. Unknown publication reconciles whole scope before repeat. Offline local work is distinct from denied writer. Confirmation routes to 007; no timeout takeover. | UIS-STATE-017–024; UIR-INFO-005/013–016; VIS-STATE-001–015 as applicable |
| WF-ANN-004 | WF-GROUP-004 / UIS-VIEW-004 | Sizing/submitting/evaluation is pending, never fill. Retained WF-027 now names submitting original request I-009, run R-014, completed decision and submitted terms; no new entry/advance until completion, acceptance/fill unconfirmed and saving separate. WF-092 remains the distinct event-evaluation endpoint. For estimate-only pending, identify draft/version and pending estimate with prior valid terms retained; do not infer a submitted request. For submission, retain original intent/terms; for evaluation, name the affected occurrence/instruction. These are compound-branch obligations, not interchangeable pending facts. Accepted waiting, effective pending, triggered, filled and cancelled name different instruction facts; a fill additionally requires recorded time/side/price/whole quantity/fees/USD. Reduced or closed position retains identity and protection outcome. Over-close and changed target reject without replacing old valid order. Known rejection permits correction; stale estimate requires current review; uncertain command reconciles the original intent (031), while unknown save preserves an already known working execution. Offline qualified reads and denied writer remain distinct. | UIS-STATE-025–032; UIR-INFO-006–011/013; VIS-STATE-001–015 as applicable |
| WF-ANN-005 | WF-GROUP-005 / UIS-VIEW-005 | Evidence pending/partial, invalid/inapplicable, unavailable offline and historical/noncurrent event each show a specific reason. Original event/time/prefix never disappears. Compatible whole-effect application blocks new decisions. Known resolved (037) and outcome unknown (077) have different actions; neither auto-plays. WF-077 explicitly disables Start bar/Play/Step as well as Sell/Buy until original publication is reconciled; the adjacent reason persists. Evidence inspection, Return paused and Reconcile publication remain enabled. Only established whole-effect resolution (037) permits manual progression. New-basis evidence uses restart review (080), never silent revaluation. An old epoch cannot resolve a new block. | UIS-STATE-033–040; UIR-INFO-006/008–011/013; VIS-STATE-001–015 as applicable |
| WF-ANN-006 | WF-GROUP-006 / UIS-VIEW-006 | Unknown freshness is not proven closure. A proven closure variant must state supporting session evidence; no receipt/weekend alone proves it. Catch-up separates requested span, qualified subset and unresolved remainder, with provisional/completed/final labels and no invented progress percentage. Empty/invalid/conflicting responses preserve old qualified catalog. Offline, rate limits and access-denied conditions expose different qualified reasons. Receipt, display lag, storage failure and unknown catalog publication do not share a success status. Replay-origin variant 048 omits later financial observations; revoked authority cannot publish. | UIS-STATE-041–048; UIR-INFO-002/012/015; VIS-STATE-001–015 as applicable |
| WF-ANN-007 | WF-GROUP-007 / UIS-VIEW-007 | 049 reviews rewind; 078 own-backup replacement; 079 layout deletion; 080 updated-basis restart. Object/run delete, reset and same-basis restart substitute the exact owned target/consequence in the same review structure; never reuse rewind suffix or updated-basis copy. Invalid input cannot be overridden. Changed version must be re-reviewed (051). Preparation allows cancellation only before publication where supported (052); known failed prepublication uses unchanged-state outcome (053), not unknown (054). New surviving generation (055) restores focus to surviving work; no automatic archive promise. | UIS-STATE-049–056; UIR-INFO-014/013/016; VIS-STATE-001–015 as applicable |
| WF-ANN-008 | WF-GROUP-008 / UIS-VIEW-008 | Report 057 and chart image 081 have distinct inclusion semantics. Empty history/absent image values state why (058); preparation/writing pins source (059); workspace advancement preserves that original snapshot (060). Known failed/incomplete artifact (061), outcome unknown (062) and verified matching complete artifact (063) differ. Unknown verifies before repeat. Local output may work offline with permitted local source; invalid/restricted disclosure (064) blocks generation and preserves source. | UIS-STATE-057–064; UIR-INFO-010/016; VIS-STATE-001–015 as applicable |

**WF-ASM status:** no new product/domain assumptions. Canvas, placement and illustrative selection are explicitly bounded composition decisions; ASM-01/ASM-02 remain the previously accepted simulation assumptions. No formula, numeric convention, provider entitlement, timeout, output option or shortcut is invented. The future run-bundle launcher is explicitly user-requested and remains disabled; it supplies no implementation authority. Section 14 prerequisites remain applicable.

### Revision 30 / WF-SET-001-r14 — entry binding propagation

This current scoped decision supersedes historical entry wording in WF-DEC-005/013/019 and WF-ANN-004. Ticket selectors prepare one shared draft; capsule Buy/Sell always submit. Only the matching currently valid direction is available. Opposite activation has no effect. The capsule retains run/instrument/completed boundary, direction/type, proposed level/SL/TP, fixed/risk inputs, specification/fees/FX basis, current estimate or superseded reason and an enabled **Edit entry / direction** route when the ticket is collapsed. No new confirmation or execution formula is introduced. WF-090 preserves A32 ticket grouping, reference-only disabled controls and sidebar bounds; its explicit Select labels distinguish preparation from replay submission.

**Historical r8 revision:** Seven existing entry pairs and seven added material variants realize explicit direction selection, matching-only submission, pending/invalid validation, fixed/risk sizing, collapsed ticket and zoom. All sources match G4-approved r14. Historical changed files and metadata are in `ui-artifacts/mockups/history/r7/`; approval-r7.json remains immutable. Independent ui-review.md revision 4 now governs the residual; its evidence is preserved.

- **High-Fidelity Set Version:** `MP-SET-001-r9`; 113 compositions / 226 SVGs, eleven revised pairs.
- **Gate G5 Approval Evidence:** user, 22 October 2026: “approve G5 mockups”. Eleven r9 pairs (068/093–095/107–113), both themes, receive renewed approval; other 102 pairs retain exact r8 bytes. [Exact current approval hashes](ui-artifacts/mockups/approval-r9.json); [revision scope](ui-artifacts/mockups/revision-r9.json).
- **Review entry:** [offline gallery](ui-artifacts/mockups/index.html); start with 107–113, then 025/068/090/093–096; compare both themes.
- **User-directed appearance:** rising candles green, falling red. Bid bodies filled with a slightly darker same-hue border/wick; Ask bodies hollow on the plot surface. Bid-left/Ask-right slots share UTC buckets; each side has its own OHLCV and volume. Toolbars match their theme background. Dark bg/fg/borders are #151619 / #D1D4DC / #2A2B30; light #FFFFFF / #1E293B / #D4DAE3. Buy buttons use dark green #14532D over light green #DCFCE7; Sell uses dark red #7F1D1D over light red #FEE2E2 in both themes. This affects appearance only; quote visibility, named calculation basis and execution-side policy stay independent. VIS-DEC-007/008 / VIS-SURF-005 / VIS-COLOR-016/017 / VIS-STATE-016 / VIS-DATA-001/009/010 govern exact values.
- **Canonical format and geometry:** self-contained SVG with system typography; HTML/CSS gallery and inert state specimens. Every mockup matches its current source rectangle/group/transform/control geometry. The documented r12 delta aligns all desktop side panels to watchlist bounds and spans status chrome across the window; command availability and contents stay intact. Baseline 1280×800; 090–092 preserve 1870×884 reference geometry; 065–072 preserve the non-normative 640×900 effective desktop-zoom illustrations. Source/mockup IDs and simulation notices stay outside the product canvas. Generated product crops remove review margins only.
- **Applied VIS rules:** VIS-TYPE-001–007, VIS-COLOR-001–017, VIS-SPACE/DENS families, VIS-SURF-001–005, VIS-STATE-001–016, VIS-DATA-001–010, VIS-ICON/MOTION/A11Y families. Financial direction is independent from application success/error and order Buy/Sell actions. Explicit open/close ticks, labelled OHLCV, filled/hollow side identity and selected checks preserve non-color reading.
- **Fixture policy:** synthetic layout/run identifiers, times, axis prices and review quantities demonstrate density; bracketed facts remain unresolved. Schematic candles/volume do not prove a numerical market path, provider units, valid financial calculation, actual trade, product default or financial result. State/recovery text from r11 is preserved: failed save differs from unknown, absent run has no replay transport, blocked/restricted actions remain disabled, and replacement success shows the surviving cursor. No missing value is turned into zero.
- **Prototype: not required.** Material transition endpoints, disclosures and effective-zoom branches are concrete numbered pairs. This review evaluates static composition/state meaning. Gallery links/details and native specimen focus allow review navigation, but do not simulate trading or prove editing, async transitions, focus restoration or adaptive runtime behavior. Those require UIV evidence at implementation; no MP-PROT runtime exists or production technology is selected.
- **Regeneration:** `python analysis/tradingview/build_mockups.py --frame 68 --frame 93 --frame 94 --frame 95 --frame 107 --frame 108 --frame 109 --frame 110 --frame 111 --frame 112 --frame 113`; rebuild viewer; `python analysis/tradingview/check_mockups.py`. [Metadata](ui-artifacts/mockups/mockup-index.json) records exact G4 source hashes, new SVG hashes, VIS hash and prior stale source hashes for provenance. An explicit design regeneration creates a new pending review round; current finalized approval is frozen separately; no captured target HTML or remote dependency is embedded.

## 20. Risk-Based UI Verification Model (`UIV-*`)

These are **required observable properties**, not claimed implementation results. Evidence IDs specify what must be collected during implementation review; Section 21 distinguishes completed design checks from evidence still required. No implementation-standards.md currently prescribes a project mechanism here. Do not infer a test runner, coverage percentage, image-diff threshold or new timing budget. Every state/adaptive row in Sections 18–19 is in scope, including compound branches documented by WF-ANN-001–008 rather than separate screen files.

| Verification ID | Category / target | Required observable property | Prohibited outcome | Governing sources / visual reference | Expected evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UIV-JRN-001 | Journey / IF-JRN-001; view 001 | Search/adopt basis, navigate all discovered history and inspect actual selected OHLC with explicit provider/side/period/time. Go to and presets move viewport, preserving actual run cursor. | 100k resident window becomes total-history cap; requested label applied to old arrays; Go to advances simulation. | UIR-TASK-001–003; UIR-ENV-003; CONTRACT-001; MP-MOCK-001/082/083/086/087 | UIV-EVID-001; UIV-EVID-002 |
| UIV-JRN-002 | Journey / IF-JRN-002; view 002 | Chart/study settings identify scope, draft, committed values, validity and distinct Apply/Cancel; cancel restores original scope and invoker. | Analysis settings alter retained simulation basis; preview presented as saved. | UIR-STATE-006; IF-REC-002; CONTRACT-002; MP-MOCK-009–016/075 | UIV-EVID-001; UIV-EVID-002 |
| UIV-JRN-003 | Journey / IF-JRN-003; view 001 | Named drawing commands and financial anchors/brush point editing allow create/select/edit/cancel/undo; hidden/locked objects remain identified. | Pointer-only drawing; measurement becomes order; resize changes financial anchor. | UIR-A11Y-003; IF-REC-003; UIS-PAT-004; MP-MOCK-006/074 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-006 |
| UIV-JRN-004 | Journey / IF-JRN-004; views 001/003 | Named layout selection, one/two-chart arrangement, active chart and explicit interval-sync choice preserve independent chart context. | Third chart, unrequested synchronization, or candidate activation on inspection. | UIR-ENV-001; UIS-ADAPT-002; CONTRACT-002/009; MP-MOCK-017/073 | UIV-EVID-001; UIV-EVID-002 |
| UIV-JRN-005 | Journey / IF-JRN-005; view 001 | Start candidate, actual run cursor, playing, paused, evaluation, blocked and actual end have named states. Play/step/speed share completed historical ordering. | Future observations or new decision past unfinished event; forced liquidation at end. | UIR-CONT-005; CONTRACT-004; ORDER-005; MP-MOCK-005/084/088/089/092 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-003 |
| UIV-JRN-006 | Journey / IF-JRN-006; views 001/007 | Rewind/restart review names retained target/version and lost trading suffix; cancel unchanged; surviving cursor explicit; drawings preserved. Restore identifies backup/included scope/completeness; deletion identifies layout/version; updated restart names selected replacement basis; completed publication offers return to surviving state, not prepublication Cancel. | Unconfirmed destructive jump or discarded drawings; updated-data basis adopted on rewind. | UIR-CONT-002; IF-CONF-001/002; CONTRACT-008; MP-MOCK-049–056/080 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-004 |
| UIV-JRN-007 | Journey / IF-JRN-007; view 002 | Units/Risk % sizing, fixed USD currency, declared commission/specification origin, required facts and planned-risk limitations remain visible. Risk-% input, required valid SL, fee-inclusive estimate and unavailable/invalid branch are distinct; invalid state disables commitment. Direction, terms, sizing, settings or context edits immediately invalidate prior validation/estimates while retaining raw inputs. New side-incompatible levels remain invalid until corrected; fresh results bind the exact draft/context; neither pending nor invalid permits submission. | Missing spec/FX becomes zero; quantity fixture becomes a default; planned risk implies max loss. | UIR-INFO-007/008; CONTRACT-011; PRE-04/05; MP-MOCK-009–016/090/095/096; IF-BR-008a; MP-MOCK-107–113 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-003 |
| UIV-JRN-008 | Journey / IF-JRN-008; view 004 | Submission identifies phase, original request/run/boundary and submitted terms; prior valid records stay readable with a reason for disabled actions. Estimate pending, submission pending (027) and event evaluation (092) stay distinct. New entry, pending-order modification and cancellation use named distinct intents, current terms and next-event eligibility; invalid edit leaves valid prior order intact. Limit/Stop expose required entry level; O-008 modification binds original terms/version and Apply modification independently from new entry. Only explicit activation of the matching validated replay Buy/Sell action submits the exact reviewed draft after validity/authority recheck. Ticket direction selection, opposite activation, typing, reopening and validation completion submit nothing; no queued click. Unresolved submission freezes original direction/terms; a new entry needs deliberate preparation. | Accepted or triggered displayed as filled; retry of unknown command creates duplicate; chart-side selection changes executable side rules. | CONTRACT-005/006; UIR-STATE-009; IDEM-001; MP-MOCK-025/027/028/029/031/076/090–094; IF-BR-008a; MP-MOCK-107–113 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-003 |
| UIV-JRN-009 | Journey / IF-JRN-009; view 004 | Close/reduce identifies a particular hedged position and current remainder; over-close/conflict has reason and refreshed review. Valid partial/full branches show positive quantity at most remainder, projected remainder/protection and named target before separate close commitment. | Opposite entry silently nets; wrong position closed; rejected close changes valid exposure. | UIR-SAFE-004; CONTRACT-005; PRE-06/08; MP-MOCK-025/029/031/097/098 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-003 |
| UIV-JRN-010 | Journey / IF-JRN-010; view 005 | Resolution retains original occurrence/time/basis/reason and coherent prefix; compatible whole effect applied once; outcome unknown reconciles original publication; no auto-play. Unknown 077 disables Start bar/Play/Step/Sell/Buy until reconciliation; known-resolved 037 permits deliberate manual progression. | Latest receipt becomes historical price, partial fill/fee/USD effect, stale epoch resolves current block. | UIR-CONT-006; CONTRACT-007; TX-006; MP-MOCK-033–040/077 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-003 |
| UIV-JRN-011 | Journey / IF-JRN-011; views 004/008 | Image and own-run report preserve pinned source version/cursor, inclusion/absence/provenance and units; verify unknown artifact before repeat; source unchanged. | Workspace advancement silently changes output, incomplete file labelled complete, empty results fabricated as zero. | CONTRACT-010; RW-008; UIR-INFO-016; MP-MOCK-057–064/081 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-005 |
| UIV-JRN-012 | Journey / IF-JRN-012; view 006 | Feed/receipt/display/publication, requested span/qualified subset/remainder and provisional/completed/final are distinguishable; local history remains readable. | Provider response proves finality/closure, invented progress/deadline, replay panel reveals later financial facts. | CONTRACT-003; CM-004/007; FAIL-004–008; MP-MOCK-041–048 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-003 |
| UIV-JRN-013 | Journey / IF-JRN-013; view 003 | Resume exact coherent checkpoint and original input closure; working and acknowledged versions visible; unknown saves reconcile without replaying known effects. | Latest catalog silently replaces retained inputs; older acknowledgement marks newer work saved; stale writer publishes. | CONTRACT-009; CM-003/005; SECR-001/006; MP-MOCK-017–024 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-004 |
| UIV-JRN-014 | Journey / IF-JRN-014; views 003/007 | Own-backup scope and integrity validated before whole replacement; exact target/version, cancel and fresh authority explicit; unrelated work preserved. | Silent merge, partial candidate activation, restored credentials or historical commands reissued. | UIR-CONT-007; CONTRACT-009; SECR-004/005; ADV-VER-001; MP-MOCK-023/049–056/078/079 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-004 |
| UIV-INT-001 | Interaction / all views | Return restores originating task/control, target, query/filter, range and selected object; dismissing a drawer does not resolve block or start playback. Entry selector and replay commit are distinct: Buy executes ask, Sell bid; opposite action is unavailable with reason and has no effect. Capsule and ticket show the same proposed conditional level and current validation context; pending/invalid edits retain that level while blocking both submissions. An absent required level blocks both with a reason. Collapsed/zoom capsule retains direction, validity, terms, run/boundary and enabled Edit entry / direction route; close restores invoker. | Context reset, obscured keyboard focus or unintended action on closing task. | IF-NAV and IF-DISC families; UIS-NAV-001–012; WF-ANN-001–008; IF-BR-008a; MP-MOCK-107–113 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-006 |

Support Bid only, Ask only, both and neither; retain axes, drawings and context with neither shown. Calculation basis, executable buy-ask/sell-bid rules and replay cursor stay unchanged. | Mutually exclusive radio/dropdown behavior, hiding a series switches calculation basis or execution side, missing keyboard/pressed-state cue, or both hidden erases chart context. | WF-DEC-015; WF-SET-001-r11; MP-MOCK-001–106 r6; MP-SPEC-001 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-006; UIV-EVID-007 |
| UIV-STRUCT-001 | Structure / all mockups | Persistent chart, left tools, top chart controls and distinct right launcher rail stay mounted. Settings, Go to and the compact replay capsule may overlay under UIS-PAT-011; other tasks reserve chart space. All side panels share watchlist top/bottom edges, below the toolbar and above the status bar; no panel covers chrome. MP-MOCK-090 retains a single capsule and collapsed results. Check every side-panel family including search, saved work, continuity, objects and quote inspection; effective-zoom bottom docks remain adaptive variants. | Panel extends over top toolbar or bottom status bar; unequal side-panel heights; standalone replacement task pages, report/rewind floating windows, future bundle launcher enabled. | UIS-PAT-006–010; MP-MOCK-001–106; UI-ART-001–106 | UIV-EVID-001; UIV-EVID-007 |

Pending submission exposes original identity, terms, retained records and unavailable-action reason inside the product surface. Each compound branch in WF-ANN is exercised. | Single generic error, false zero, failure substituted for unknown or erased coherent evidence. | UIS-STATE-001–064; VIS-STATE-001–015; FAIL-001–023 | UIV-EVID-003; UIV-EVID-007 |

Known failed retention (015) uses an error mark and VIS-COLOR-013, names working v13 / failed save v13 / retained v12 and retries retention only. Unknown publication uses a question mark and VIS-COLOR-014, reconciles the original occurrence and blocks further mutation/advance (077), distinct from known-resolved 037. | Saved success inferred from fill, rollback inferred from missing acknowledgement, blind repeat of command/replacement. | UIS-PAT-001/003; CM-003/006; FAIL-012/013/017; MP-MOCK-007/015/020/021/031/037/054/077 | UIV-EVID-003; UIV-EVID-004 |

Keyboard reaches selector, fields, validation, terms and separate matching commit; editor restores invoker and Enter never implicitly submits. | Pointer-only plot task or keyboard trap; static SVG role=img used as product accessibility implementation. | UIR-A11Y-001/003/004; VIS-A11Y-002/003; MP-MOCK-074; MP-SPEC-001; IF-BR-008a; MP-MOCK-107–113 | UIV-EVID-002; UIV-EVID-006 |

No tenancy/login workflow is introduced: this is the approved local analyst/writer-authority model. Restriction coverage comes from existing SECR and UIS states, not invented TEN requirements. Project mechanisms are governed by implementation-standards.md IS-QA-001; these static checks do not supply runtime evidence.

**INT-002 evidence expectations:** UIV-EVID-002/003 must exercise ready Buy and Sell with opposite-action attempts (zero effect), fixed/risk edits, direction changes with retained invalid levels, stale validation responses, matching-submit recheck, pending/unknown freeze, deliberate new entry and distinct order modification/close. UIV-EVID-006/007 must cover keyboard direction/status announcements, no implicit Enter submit, readable disabled reasons, editor focus restoration and collapse/zoom continuity. These are required future runtime observations, not static design passes.

### Observed design checks (separate from runtime evidence)

r9 adds direct capsule/ticket proposed-level comparison for all eleven conditional compositions in both themes, including type/direction/identity, ready validity and pending/invalid restrictions. Exact scope and unchanged hashes are in revision-r9.json. Fresh renders replace all 22 changed canonical PNGs and 22 product PNGs; render-checks.json records their identities. This is authored-design evidence, not runtime UIV-EVID completion.

[Design check record](ui-artifacts/mockups/review-checks.json) records actual artifact checks against exact current source hashes: current r11/r12/r13/r14 mapping, rectangle/group/control geometry and availability, semantic quote groups, direction/edge tokens, theme toolbar surface/foreground scope and Buy/Sell fill/text roles, XML safety and local links. Retained prior source hashes record historical drift; no stale source pair is silently counted current. Historical G4 approval file is read and never rewritten. During revision, pending sources must match separate revision.json hashes; finalized current geometry acceptance is frozen in approval-r12.json, approval-r13.json and approval-r14.json. r8 checks verify matching-only entry availability, current direction/invalidity reasons and editor access; retained r7 checks also verify known-failure cues, pending-submission feedback, unknown-publication restrictions and retained r6 approval hashes. All desktop side panels are detected by outer position/bounds, including the ungrouped watchlist, instead of checking only named trading panels.

**Historical r8 rendering scope:** Rendered exports and visual-inspection scope were recorded in [render-checks.json](ui-artifacts/mockups/render-checks.json): all 226 current canonical SVGs have matching PNG exports; 78 product crops have PNG exports. All fourteen affected compositions were inspected in both themes using product contact sheets, including explicit direction, opposite-action reasons, pending/invalid estimates, the error cue in 096/109, collapsed editor in 110 and zoom docks in 068/111. Unchanged renders were reused only when their source digest matched. Gallery/viewer source and local links were checked; browser layout/focus behavior remains unobserved in this revision. PNGs are derived review aids; canonical files remain SVG. Static evidence establishes authored artifact consistency only. Keyboard task completion, actual async transitions, focus restoration, runtime contrast/reflow, provider behavior, calculations, durability and performance remain expected UIV-EVID evidence, not observed passes. Project tooling is prescribed by IS-QA-001; no new test mechanism or acceptance threshold is selected here.

## 23. AI UI Implementation Handoff

### Authority precedence and staleness

Requirements/domain/architecture retain semantic authority. For UI realization, apply architecture-dossier.md → Part I UIR → Part II IF → Part III UIS → visual-system.md VIS → Part IV approved WF → Part V MP/UIV. A lower-level picture cannot override an upper-level guarantee. MP-SET-001-r9 matches all 113 approved r14 sources. Eleven corrected conditional-entry pairs have renewed G5 approval; 102 unchanged pairs retain exact approval. The revision-29 INT-002 UX contract is propagated; independent review remains OPEN until recheck. If governing UIR/IF/UIS/VIS or approved geometry changes, list affected UI-ART entries as `STALE_VISUAL_ARTIFACT` under UISYN-GAP, invalidate relevant G4/G5 approval and revise/reapprove before implementation relies on them. Source SVG hashes in mockup-index.json detect geometry-source drift; they do not substitute for semantic change review.

### Non-negotiable constraints (`UI-IMPL-*`)

| ID | Category | Non-negotiable rule | Source IDs |
|---|---|---|---|
| UI-IMPL-001 | Semantics / authority | Preserve upstream business meaning and unresolved DEFER decisions; no fixture or reference screenshot creates a feature or numeric default | UIR scope; Section 18; DEFER-001–007 |
| UI-IMPL-002 | Structure | Retain chart-led shell and scoped side/bottom tasks; floating exceptions are Settings, Go to and the replay capsule (UIS-PAT-011); future bundle launcher disabled | UIS-PAT-006–010; UI-ART-001–106; UIV-STRUCT-001 |
| UI-IMPL-003 | Flow / boundary | Every replay read/watchlist/result/output respects common actual cursor and retained inputs; viewport/crosshair/candidate never redefines it | UIS-PAT-002/010; UIR-CONT-005; UIV-JRN-001/005/010/012 |
| UI-IMPL-004 | State truthfulness | Distinguish accepted, executed, working, retained and artifact outcomes; known failed retention uses error cues and retention-only retry; unknown uses uncertainty cues and reconciles original occurrence without duplicate effect. 027 shows pending submission scope/terms and blocking reason; 077 blocks advance/mutation pending reconciliation, distinct from resolved 037 | VIS-STATE-006/008/014/015; UIV-STATE-001–003 |
| UI-IMPL-005 | Consequence / recovery | Exact target/version/consequence plus cancel/confirm remain co-present; changed version invalidates review; known unchanged and unknown differ | IF-CONF-001–003; UIV-JRN-006/014; UIV-STATE-003 |
| UI-IMPL-006 | Trading / targets | Distinct entry/modify/cancel/target-close actions; whole execution identity and remaining quantity; no decisions past unfinished event; risk estimate limitations readable. Explicit ticket direction selection never submits; only matching currently validated replay action submits after recheck. Opposite action has no effect. Edits invalidate validation/estimates; pending/invalid blocks both. Capsule and ticket must share the proposed Limit/Stop level and validation context. Retain proposed levels during pending/invalid validation; absent required level disables both submissions with a reason. Preserve direction/readback/editor through collapse/zoom; restore focus. Freeze unresolved submitted terms; retain distinct modification/cancel/close intents. | IF-BR-008a; UIV-JRN-007/008; UIV-INT-001; UIV-ADAPT-001; UIV-A11Y-001; MP-MOCK-025/068/090/093–096/107–113 |
| UI-IMPL-007 | Visual references / tokens | After G5 approval, match the applicable paired-theme MP/UI-ART composition and VIS semantic roles. Preserve neutral/status/action/financial distinctions and redundant cues | Section 18/19; UIV-VIS-001; UIV-DATA-001/002 |
| UI-IMPL-008 | Accessibility | Implement named semantic controls and editable numeric alternatives; keyboard completion, focus, target sizes, enlargement and both-theme contrast to accepted WCAG 2.2 AA scope | UIR-A11Y-001–006; UIV-A11Y-001–003; MP-SPEC-001 |
| UI-IMPL-009 | Adaptation | Preserve task hierarchy, target/terms/reason/actions under text enlargement; scroll task bodies with persistent reachable actions; never shrink critical text to reproduce SVG dimensions | UIS-ADAPT-001–006; UI-ART-065–072; UIV-ADAPT-001 |
| UI-IMPL-010 | Output / continuity | Pin source before generation; scope and absence labels survive source advance; verify unknown artifact; no source mutation or secret/private evidence disclosure | UIV-JRN-011; UIV-CONT-001; UIV-ACC-001 |
| UI-IMPL-011 | Verification | Link each implemented slice/view/state to relevant UIV properties and actual evidence; separate artifact integrity from runtime behavior, performance, accessibility and durability | Sections 20/21; ADV-VER-001 |
| UI-IMPL-012 | Typography / data | Use canonical type roles, explicit units, tabular comparables and readable reasons; actual financial data replaces fixture examples only through governed contracts | VIS-TYPE-001–007; VIS-DATA-001–010; UIV-VIS-001; UIV-DATA-001/002 |
| UI-IMPL-013 | Quote identity / calculation | Bid filled and Ask hollow; both-visible paired UTC slots, separate OHLCV and provider volume. Keep visibility independent from explicit Settings > Symbol calculation basis and executable sides; both-hide keeps context. | UIS-PAT-012; VIS-DATA-009/010; UIV-INT-002/003; UIV-DATA-003 |
| UI-IMPL-014 | User visual override | Rising green, falling red, darker same-color filled Bid borders/wicks; toolbar backgrounds/foregrounds/borders match exact active-theme neutrals. Buy uses #14532D on #DCFCE7; Sell #7F1D1D on #FEE2E2 in both themes; disabled/selected states retain independent marks. | VIS-DEC-008; VIS-SURF-005; VIS-COLOR-016/017; VIS-STATE-016; VIS-DATA-010; UIV-VIS-002 |
| UI-IMPL-015 | Folder scope / prerequisites | Settings Data location distinguishes active/draft/validation/adoption. Never mutate sibling source files or silently rebind saved-run inputs. Implement only after architecture ownership/validation/publication/catalog-adoption contract is supplied. | UIR-TASK-020; IF-JRN-015; UIV-JRN-015; UISYN-MOD-004 |
| UI-IMPL-016 | Side-panel bounds | Every desktop side panel shares the watchlist's y=top-toolbar-bottom and bottom=status-bar-top. Panel content scrolls/clips within its own body; panel chrome cannot cover workspace chrome. Existing bottom-dock zoom variants remain docks. | UIS-PAT-013; UIV-STRUCT-001; UISYN-MOD-005 |

### Explicit implementation freedom (`UI-FREEDOM-*`)

| ID | Delegated local choice | Boundary / conditions |
|---|---|---|
| UI-FREEDOM-001 | Platform, framework, component/state organization and test mechanisms | Standards-architect owns project choices; preserve approved hierarchy, semantics and UIV evidence obligations |
| UI-FREEDOM-002 | Responsive layout mechanics and internal scrolling realization | Match approved structure/context at baseline and effective zoom; do not infer 640px as mandatory breakpoint or mobile scope |
| UI-FREEDOM-003 | Accessible chart rendering and equivalent numeric editing mechanisms | Plot technology is open; retain financial coordinates, readable alternatives, keyboard capability and performance authority |
| UI-FREEDOM-004 | Component composition and local control sizing/wrapping | Preserve ordering/co-presence, canonical type roles, target sizes and reachability; SVG rectangles are design references, not fixed clipping limits |
| UI-FREEDOM-005 | Internal formatting implementation and unobtrusive decorative details | No changes to approved precision/units/semantics, exact role palette or task geometry; numerical conventions require their owning DEFER resolution |

### Prohibited inference and deferred UI decisions (`UI-DEFER-*`)

- **UI-DEFER-001 — Static artifact non-authority:** never copy SVG DOM, gallery HTML, synthetic fixtures, inert specimens or generator file organization as production architecture, event ordering, persistence, routing or state management. No product prototype exists. Gallery links/details are review navigation only.
- **UI-DEFER-002 — Financial/sample inference:** do not turn sample instrument, dates, quantity, balance, fees or OHLC into selected startup defaults, forecasts, numerical conventions or validated outputs. Drawings are analysis; schematic candle order is not intrabar execution evidence.
- **UI-DEFER-003 — Existing prerequisites:** DEFER-001/002 govern provider/session/finality evidence; DEFER-003 tool/study/step/output conventions; DEFER-004 monetary conventions; DEFER-005 bounded UX defaults; DEFER-006 retry/timing configuration; DEFER-007 technical realization. ADV-VER-001 remains persistence-race verification. Resolve applicable prerequisites before affected implementation; this handoff does not settle them.
- **UI-DEFER-004 — Scope exclusions:** no broker execution, crypto catalog import, scripting, alerts, external run-bundle import, enabled future viewer, additional color-vision theme, mobile mandate, account/credential interface or new provider behavior inferred from references.
- **UI-DEFER-005 — Gate discipline:** G4 is satisfied for all 113 r14 sources. G5 is satisfied for eleven renewed r9 pairs; 102 unchanged pairs retain exact approval-r8.json hashes; historical approvals retained. Historical approvals do not transfer to changed bytes; independent ui-adversary review follows scoped G5.
